Our work helps to establish a novel type of certified RAG systems for a broad range of natural language industry applications.

</details>


### [145] [TriAlignGR: Triangular Multitask Alignment with Multimodal Deep Interest Mining for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05249)
*Yangchen Zeng,Hao Peng,Rongfeng Guo,Zhenyu Yu,Zhiyuan Hu,Jinze Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TriAlignGR是一个统一的多任务多模态生成式推荐框架，通过两阶段多模态语义传播（视觉语义编码到SID + 视觉描述任务解码）解决SID内容退化（SCD）和SID语义不透明（SSO）两大问题，包含跨模态语义对齐、多模态深度兴趣挖掘和三角多任务训练三个核心组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SID管道存在两个未被充分探索的根本问题：一是级联编码和残差量化丢弃了关键的多模态和兴趣级语义（SCD），二是模型自回归生成SID序列时并不真正理解其底层含义，导致幻觉和泛化能力差。先前工作最多只涉及文本-SID对齐，视觉语义和潜在用户兴趣完全未被利用。

Method: TriAlignGR包含三个紧密集成的组件：(1) CMSA通过VLM生成的文本描述和多模态嵌入模型直接将视觉内容整合到SID构建中；(2) MDIM利用LLM链式思维推理提取潜在用户意图以丰富SID语义；(3) TMT在单一自回归损失下联合训练八个互补生成任务，包括两个新颖的视觉语义任务（VisDesc→SID, VisDesc→Title），无需特定任务塔或复杂损失加权。

Result: 通过两阶段多模态语义传播，SID序列同时承载多模态语义和用户兴趣信息；TMT联合训练框架构建了SID-文本-图像三角关系，无需额外任务塔或损失加权。

Conclusion: TriAlignGR通过统一多任务多模态框架有效解决了SCD和SSO两大问题，将视觉语义和用户兴趣注入SID，提升了生成式推荐的泛化能力和语义理解水平。

Abstract: We introduce TriAlignGR, a unified multitask-multimodal framework for generative recommendation that establishes two-stage multimodal semantic propagation: (i) encoding visual semantics directly into SIDs via multimodal embeddings, and (ii) enabling the model to decode these semantics through visual description tasks. Existing Semantic ID (SID) pipelines suffer from two fundamental but underexplored problems: \textbf{SID Content Degradation (SCD)}, where cascaded encoding and residual quantization discard critical multimodal and interest-level semantics; and \textbf{SID Semantic Opacity (SSO)}, where models autoregressively generate SID sequences without truly comprehending their underlying meaning, leading to hallucination and poor generalization. Prior work addresses at most text-SID alignment, leaving visual semantics and latent user interests entirely unexploited. TriAlignGR resolves both problems through three tightly integrated components: (1)~\textbf{Cross-Modal Semantic Alignment (CMSA)} integrates visual content into SID construction through both VLM-generated textual descriptions and a multimodal embedding model that directly encodes image features alongside text, ensuring that SIDs inherently carry multimodal semantics; (2)~\textbf{Multimodal Deep Interest Mining (MDIM)} leverages LLM Chain-of-Thought reasoning to extract latent user intents (\eg ``productivity-focused lifestyle'' from noise-canceling headphones) beyond surface attributes, enriching SID semantics before discretization; and (3)~\textbf{Triangular Multitask (TMT)} jointly trains on eight complementary generation tasks under a single autoregressive loss -- including two novel visual-semantic tasks (VisDesc$\to$SID, VisDesc$\to$Title) that map VLM-generated image descriptions to SIDs and titles, completing the SID-Text-Image triangle -- without requiring task-specific towers or complex loss weighting.

</details>


### [146] [Decision-aware User Simulation Agent for Evaluating Conversational Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05250)
*Yuan-Chi Li,Li-Chi Chen,Sung-Yi Wu,Yu-Che Tsai,Shou-De Lin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出Hesitator框架，通过显式建模选择过载下的决策过程（分离效用评估与承诺决策），使对话推荐系统中的用户模拟器更真实地模拟人类犹豫和延迟决策行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用户模拟器（尤其是基于LLM的）未能显式建模内部决策过程，表现出不切实际的信息处理能力，很少展现真实消费者常见的犹豫或决策延迟，导致接受概率过高，无法准确评估对话推荐系统。

Method: 提出Hesitator框架，包含模块化决策模块（Decision Module），将基于效用的商品选择与考虑过载的承诺决策相分离；在多种模拟框架、领域、销售模式和LLM后端上进行实验验证。

Result: 集成该模块后，在不断增加过载条件下一致地缓解了不真实行为；重现了心理经济学中已建立的行为模式。

Conclusion: Hesitator能够有效建模人类决策行为，使对话推荐系统的用户模拟更加真实可靠。

Abstract: Conversational recommender systems (CRS) increasingly rely on user simulators for automated evaluation of sales agents. A key requirement for such simulators is the ability to model human decision-making. However, most existing simulation frameworks do not explicitly model the internal decision process, and LLM-based simulators often exhibit unrealistically strong information-processing capabilities, rarely exhibit the hesitation or decision deferral commonly observed in real consumer behavior, resulting in overly high acceptance probabilities. To address this limitation, we propose Hesitator, a theory-grounded user simulation framework that explicitly models human decision-making under choice overload. The framework introduces a modular Decision Module that separates utility-based item selection from overload-aware commitment decisions. Experiments across multiple user simulation frameworks, domains, sales modes, and LLM backbones show that integrating our module consistently mitigates unrealistic behaviors under increasing overload conditions. Furthermore, Hesitator reproduces established behavioral patterns from psychological economics, demonstrating its ability to model human decision behavior.

</details>


### [147] [Career-Aware Resume Tailoring via Multi-Source Retrieval-Augmented Generation with Provenance Tracking: A Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05257)
*Kumar Abhinav*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Resume Tailor，一种基于智能体的简历定制系统，通过维护纵向职业库（向量数据库）并利用多源检索增强生成（RAG）技术，从历史简历和结构化职业记录中组装职位特定内容。系统采用12节点LangGraph流水线、混合语义-词汇置信度评分、溯源感知回退生成、反幻觉护栏及条件审查循环。对9个职位描述（涵盖软件工程、数据分析、业务分析）的初步评估显示：当候选人在同一职业类别有先验经验时，ATS风格匹配分数平均提升7.8分；而领域知识缺失时分数平均下降8.0分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI辅助简历定制系统仅基于单次上传的简历运行，限制了从当前草稿中恢复遗漏相关经验的能力，且用户难以区分基于真实经历的编辑与模型生成的建议。

Method: 构建12节点LangGraph流水线，包含类型化状态管理、混合语义-词汇置信度评分、溯源感知回退生成、反幻觉护栏及条件审查循环。核心创新在于维护纵向职业库（向量数据库），采用多源RAG从历史简历和结构化职业记录中检索并组装职位特定简历内容。

Result: 对同一候选人的9个职位描述（涵盖软件工程、数据分析、业务分析）进行初步评估：在6个候选人有同职业类别先验经验的职位中，启用职业库后ATS风格匹配分数平均提升7.8分；2个领域知识缺失的职位分数平均下降8.0分；1个部分重叠职位提升2分。

Conclusion: 纵向检索能在存在相关先验经验时有效改进简历定制效果，同时也凸显了在领域重叠较弱时需要基于置信度门控的检索策略。

Abstract: AI-assisted resume tailoring systems commonly operate on a single uploaded resume, which limits their ability to recover relevant experience omitted from the current draft and makes it difficult for users to distinguish grounded edits from model-generated suggestions. This paper presents Resume Tailor, an agentic resume-tailoring system that maintains a longitudinal career vault in a vector database and uses multi-source retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) to assemble job-specific resume content from historical resumes and structured career records. The system is implemented as a 12-node LangGraph pipeline with typed state management, hybrid semantic-lexical confidence scoring, provenance-aware fallback generation, anti-hallucination guardrails, and a conditional review loop. We report a pilot evaluation on nine job descriptions (JDs) across software engineering, data analytics, and business analysis roles using a single candidate's career history. For six JDs where the candidate held at least one prior role in the same occupational category, enabling the career vault improved Applicant Tracking System (ATS)-style fit scores by an average of 7.8 points. For two JDs requiring domain-specific expertise absent from the vault, scores decreased by an average of 8.0 points. One partially overlapping role showed a modest gain of 2 points. These results suggest that longitudinal retrieval can improve resume tailoring when relevant prior experience exists, while also highlighting the need for confidence-gated retrieval when domain overlap is weak.

</details>


### [148] [Effective Knowledge Transfer for Multi-Task Recommendation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05730)
*Guohao Cai,Jun Yuan,Zhenhua Dong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出EKTM方法，通过路由模块和发射器模块实现多任务推荐模型中的知识迁移，从而缓解CVR预测中正样本稀疏问题，在离线基准和在线A/B测试中均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CVR（转化率）是衡量平台效果的关键指标，但由于用户转化行为稀疏，训练有效的排序模型面临严峻挑战。现有方法难以充分利用多任务间的关联知识来提升CVR预测性能。

Method: 提出EKTM方法，包含三个核心模块：(1) 路由模块，负责跨任务的知识整合与分发；(2) 发射器模块，为每个CVR任务配备，将路由器的知识转化为任务特定形式；(3) 增强模块，确保迁移的知识助力原任务学习。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上优于现有SOTA方法；在商业平台的大规模在线A/B测试中，有效Cost Per Mille (eCPM)提升3.93%，算法已全量部署至平台两个主要流量场景。

Conclusion: EKTM通过有效的知识迁移机制，使多任务推荐模型能够从不同用户行为中学习，显著缓解了CVR稀疏性问题，并在工业大规模场景中验证了实用价值。

Abstract: The conversion rate (CVR) is a crucial metric for evaluating the effectiveness of platforms, as it quantifies the alignment of content with audience preferences. However, the limited nature of customers' conversion actions presents a significant challenge for training ranking models effectively. In this paper, we propose an Effective Knowledge Transfer method for Multi-task Recommendation Models (EKTM). This method enables the ranking model to learn from diverse user behaviors, thereby enhancing performance through the transfer of knowledge across distinct yet related tasks. Each specific CVR task can directly benefit from the insights provided by other tasks. To achieve this, we first introduce a router module that integrates and disseminates knowledge across tasks. Subsequently, each CVR task is equipped with a transmitter module that facilitates the transformation of knowledge from the router. Additionally, we propose an enhanced module to ensure that the transferred knowledge benefit the original task learning. Extensive experiments on several benchmark datasets demonstrate that our proposed method outperforms existing state-of-the-art approaches. Online A/B testing on a commercial platform has validated the effectiveness of the EKTM algorithm in large-scale industrial settings, resulting in a 3.93% uplift in effective Cost Per Mille (eCPM). The algorithm has since been fully deployed across two of the platform's main-traffic scenarios.

</details>


### [149] [Beyond Long Tail POIs: Transition-Centered Generalization for Human Mobility Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05771)
*Dingyang Lyu,Zhengjia Xu,Jey Han Lau,Jianzhong Qi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出RECAP框架，通过组合泛化解决人类移动预测中转移级长尾问题，利用多跳传递性和用户历史重访证据重建长尾转移，并采用热转移留出训练抑制记忆、鼓励泛化，在多个数据集上提升预测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究关注POI级长尾问题，但分析发现即使热门POI的预测也常失败，根本原因是转移级稀疏性（源-目标转移在训练集中很少或从未出现）。作者认为人类移动预测的核心瓶颈在于转移级长尾泛化。

Method: 将问题形式化为组合泛化，提出RECAP框架：(1) 从全局转移图中的多跳传递性和用户历史轨迹中的重访证据重建长尾转移；(2) 采用热转移留出训练策略，阻止模型记忆频繁转移，鼓励从可迁移信号中泛化。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，RECAP持续提升预测准确率，在尾转移上提升尤为明显。

Conclusion: 人类移动预测的核心瓶颈是转移级长尾泛化，而非POI级长尾；通过组合泛化视角和相应的重建框架可有效缓解该问题。

Abstract: Human mobility prediction forecasts a user's next Point of Interest (POI) from historical trajectories, supporting applications from recommendation to urban planning. Recent studies have recognized the problem with long-tail POIs in human mobility prediction, which are POIs with few visit records, making new visits to such POIs difficult to predict. Our analysis shows that many predictions fail even for visits to popular POIs. The underlying cause is often transition-level sparsity: the corresponding source-destination transition appears rarely, or never appears, in the training set. We therefore argue that a core bottleneck in human mobility prediction lies in transition-level long-tail generalization. We formulate this problem as compositional generalization and propose a tRansition rEconstruction framework for Compositional generAlization in next-POI prediction (RECAP). RECAP reconstructs long-tail transitions from two generalizable signals: multi-hop transitivity in the global transition graph and revisit evidence from a user's historical trajectory. It further uses warm-transition holdout training to discourage memorization of frequent transitions and encourage generalization from transferable signals. Experiments on multiple real-world datasets show that RECAP consistently improves prediction accuracy, with clear gains on tail transitions.

</details>


### [150] [Unified Value Alignment for Generative Recommendation in Industrial Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05803)
*Xinxun Zhang,Yuling Xiong,Jiale Zhou,Zhengkai Guo,Zhennan Pang,Junbang Huo,Jingwen Wang,Xuyang Sun,Enming Zhang,Jiaguang Jin,Changping Wang,Yi Li,Jun Zhang,Xiao Yan,Jiawei Jiang,Jie Jiang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: UniVA提出了一种统一价值对齐框架，通过商业SID分词器、生成即排序的解码器以及价值引导的波束搜索，将商业价值信号融入生成式推荐的全流程，在广告推荐场景中实现了显著的收入提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐（GR）方法主要关注语义匹配，难以将商业价值信号（如eCPM）对齐到分词、解码和在线推理等各个环节，导致在工业广告场景中无法同时优化用户兴趣和商业价值。

Method: 1) 提出Commercial SID分词器，将价值相关属性注入SID构建中；2) 提出Generation-as-Ranking SID解码器，结合监督学习和eCPM感知的强化学习联合优化；3) 设计价值引导的个性化波束搜索，复用解码logits作为在线价值引导，并使用个性化字典树约束解码到合法路径。

Result: 在腾讯微信视频号广告平台上，离线Hit Rate@100提升37.04%，在线A/B测试中GMV提升1.5%。

Conclusion: UniVA有效解决了生成式推荐在广告场景中商业价值对齐的难题，验证了将价值信号融入GR各环节的可行性，并在工业大规模系统中取得了显著效果。

Abstract: Generative Recommendation (GR) reformulates recommendation as a next-token generation problem and has shown promise in industrial applications. However, extending GR to industrial advertising is non-trivial because the system must optimize not only user interest but also commercial value. Existing GR pipelines remain largely semantics-centric, making it difficult to align value signals across tokenization, decoding, and online serving. To address this issue, we propose UniVA, a Unified Value Alignment framework for advertising recommendation. We first introduce a Commercial SID tokenizer that injects value-related attributes into SID construction, yielding value-discriminative item representations. We then develop a Generation-as-Ranking SID Decoder jointly optimized by supervised learning and eCPM-aware reinforcement learning, which fuses value scores into next-item SID generation to perform generation and ranking in one decoding process. Finally, we design a value-guided personalized beam search that reuses generation-as-ranking logits as online value guidance and applies a personalized trie tree to constrain decoding to request-valid SID paths. Experiments on the Tencent WeChat Channels advertising platform show that UniVA achieves a 37.04\% improvement in offline Hit Rate@100 over the baseline and a 1.5\% GMV lift in online A/B tests.

</details>


### [151] [Bridging Passive and Active: Enhancing Conversation Starter Recommendation via Active Expression Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05855)
*Yiqing Wu,Haoming Li,Guanyu Jiang,Jiahao Liang,Yongchun Zhu,Jingwu Chen,Feng Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出PA-Bridge框架，通过对抗分布对齐器桥接被动推荐与主动表达之间的分布差异，并引入语义离散化器实现流行度去偏，在会话搜索的对话启动器推荐中打破传统"曝光-点击"反馈循环的确认偏误，显著提升特征渗透率和用户活跃天数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对话启动器推荐依赖"曝光-点击"反馈循环，导致系统陷入信息茧房，偏向流行但通用的建议，无法捕捉开放世界中会话搜索意图的动态变化。用户主动输入的自由文本查询蕴含的开放世界意图是打破该循环的关键，但存在分布偏移和文本非ID化带来的工业级流行度统计困难两大挑战。

Method: 提出Passive-Active Bridge (PA-Bridge)框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 对抗分布对齐器(Adversarial Distribution Aligner)，用于桥接被动推荐启动器与用户主动表达之间的分布差异；(2) 语义离散化器(Semantic Discretizer)，将开放文本转化为可处理的形式，从而部署流行度去偏算法。

Result: 在线A/B测试结果表明，PA-Bridge在平台上显著提升了特征渗透率0.54%和用户活跃天数。

Conclusion: 通过利用用户的主动表达（自由意志），可以有效打破传统推荐系统中的反馈循环，实现更个性化、更动态的对话启动器推荐。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-driven conversational search is shifting information retrieval from reactive keyword matching to proactive, open-ended dialogues. In this context, Conversation Starters are widely deployed to provide personalized query recommendations that help users initiate dialogues. Conventionally, recommending these starters relies on a closed "exposure-click" loop. Yet, this feedback loop mechanism traps the system in an echo chamber where, compounded by data sparsity, it fails to capture the dynamic nature of conversational search intents shaped by the open world. As a result, the system skews towards popular but generic suggestions.In this work, we uncover an untapped paradigm shift to shatter this harmful feedback loop: harnessing user "free will" through active user expressions. Unlike traditional recommendations, conversational search empowers users to bypass menus entirely through manually typed queries. The open-world intents in active queries hold the key to breaking this loop. However, incorporating them is non-trivial: (1) there exists an inherent distribution shift between active queries and formulated starters. (2) Furthermore, the "non-ID-able" nature of open text renders traditional item-based popularity statistics ineffective for large-scale industrial streaming training. To this end, we propose Passive-Active Bridge (PA-Bridge), a novel framework that employs an adversarial distribution aligner to bridge the distributional gap between passively recommended starters and active expressions. Moreover, we introduce a semantic discretizer to enable the deployment of popularity debiasing algorithms. Online A/B tests on our platform, demonstrate that PA-Bridge significantly boosts the Feature Penetration Rate by 0.54% and User Active Days

</details>


### [152] [A Case-Driven Multi-Agent Framework for E-Commerce Search Relevance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05991)
*Global E-Commerce Search Relevance Team*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于多智能体的案例驱动框架，用自主Agent替代传统电商搜索相关性优化中的人类角色（标注、优化、评估），实现从坏案例识别到解决的全自动化闭环，并通过工程化设计提升了标注准确率和案例解决效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电商搜索相关性优化涉及用户、产品经理、标注员、算法工程师、评估员等多个人类角色，流程复杂且效率低下。本文旨在探索能否用自主智能体替代这些人类角色，构建一个自动化、持续演进的生态系统。

Method: 提出一个案例驱动的多智能体框架，包含三个核心Agent：Annotator Agent（多轮标注）、Optimizer Agent（自主分析并解决坏案例）、User Agent（通过对话交互识别坏案例）。工程上采用harness-engineering范式，构建了统一检索排序模型、指令跟随模型、全局内存机制、深度搜索Agent和人机协作聊天机器人。

Result: 大量人工评估表明，该框架能有效执行相关性相关任务，提升标注准确率，实现更及时和更具泛化性的坏案例解决。

Conclusion: 该框架为工业搜索相关性优化提供了一种实用的新范式，证明了用多智能体系统替代传统人类角色的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Relevance is a foundation of user experience in e-commerce search. We view relevance optimization as a closed-loop ecosystem involving multiple human roles: users who provide feedback, product managers who define standards, annotators who label data, algorithm engineers who optimize models, and evaluators who assess performance. Because improving relevance in practice means systematically resolving user-perceived bad cases, we ask a system-level question: can this ecosystem be reimagined by replacing its human roles with autonomous agents? To answer this question, we propose a case-driven multi-agent framework that automates the pipeline from bad-case identification to resolution. The framework instantiates an Annotator Agent for multi-turn annotation, an Optimizer Agent for autonomous bad-case analysis and resolution, and a User Agent that identifies bad cases through conversational interaction, together forming an autonomous and continually evolving system. To make the framework practical in production, we further adopt a harness-engineering paradigm and build a unified retrieval-and-ranking relevance model for efficient training, an instruction-following relevance model for real-time case resolution, Global Memory to reduce information asymmetry across agents, a Deep Search Agent to target underestimation failures, and an agent-based chatbot for human--agent collaboration. Extensive human evaluation shows that the framework performs relevance-related tasks effectively, improves annotation accuracy, and enables more timely and generalizable bad-case resolution, indicating a practical paradigm for industrial search relevance optimization.

</details>


### [153] [OBLIQ-Bench: Exposing Overlooked Bottlenecks in Modern Retrievers with Latent and Implicit Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06235)
*Diane Tchuindjo,Devavrat Shah,Omar Khattab*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出"斜向查询"（oblique queries）概念，指寻找隐含潜在模式文档的查询（如立场、失败模式、抽象场景）。构建OBLIQ-Bench基准（5个问题），揭示检索与验证的不对称性：推理LLM能识别已召回的潜在相关文档，但现有检索pipeline无法有效召回相关文档。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索基准日趋饱和，但高效搜索远未解决。存在一类被忽视的"斜向查询"，其检索具有根本性挑战。

Method: 定义斜向查询及其三种产生机制，构建包含5个斜向搜索问题的OBLIQ-Bench基准，在真实长尾语料上进行评估。

Result: 推理LLM能可靠识别已召回的潜在相关文档，但即使是最复杂的检索pipeline也无法召回大部分相关文档，揭示了检索与验证之间的不对称性。

Conclusion: OBLIQ-Bench将推动能高效捕获大规模语料中潜在模式和隐式信号的检索架构研究。

Abstract: Retrieval benchmarks are increasingly saturating, but we argue that efficient search is far from a solved problem. We identify a class of queries we call oblique, which seek documents that instantiate a latent pattern, like finding all tweets that express an implicit stance, chat logs that demonstrate a particular failure mode, or transcripts that match an abstract scenario. We study three mechanisms through which obliqueness may arise and introduce OBLIQ-Bench, a suite of five oblique search problems over real long-tail corpora. OBLIQ-Bench exposes an overlooked asymmetry between retrieval and verification, where reasoning LLMs reliably recognize latent relevance whenever relevant documents are surfaced, but even sophisticated retrieval pipelines fail to surface most relevant documents in the first place. We hope that OBLIQ-Bench will drive research into retrieval architectures that efficiently capture latent patterns and implicit signals in large corpora.

</details>


### [154] [Expressiveness Limits of Autoregressive Semantic ID Generation in Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06331)
*Yupeng Hou,Haven Kim,Clark Mingxuan Ju,Eduardo Escoto,Neil Shah,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文发现生成式推荐模型在自回归解码时存在树结构导致物品概率耦合的问题，使得模型难以区分语义相近的物品。为此提出Latte方法，通过注入潜在令牌将解码空间从单树扩展为多树，平均提升3.45%的NDCG@10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐模型的自回归解码过程会引入结构化的解码空间，其树状结构导致物品概率与树距离强相关，限制了模型的表达能力，使模型难以根据用户偏好区分树中相近的物品。

Method: 提出Latte方法，在语义ID之前注入一个潜在令牌（latent token），将原本单一的解码树重塑为多个由潜在令牌条件化的子树，从而在物品之间创建多条不同树距离的路径，松弛由树结构导致的概率耦合。

Result: 在NDCG@10指标上平均提升3.45%，验证了Latte缓解树结构概率耦合问题的有效性。

Conclusion: 生成式推荐模型的解码树结构会引入概率耦合，限制模型表达力；Latte通过注入潜在令牌重构解码空间，有效缓解该问题并提升推荐性能。

Abstract: Generative recommendation (GR) models generate items by autoregressively producing a sequence of discrete tokens that jointly index the target item. However, this autoregressive generation process also induces a structured decoding space whose impact on model expressiveness remains underexplored. Specifically, token-by-token generation can be viewed as traversing a decoding tree induced by semantic ID tokens, where leaf nodes correspond to candidate items. We observe that the item probabilities produced by GR models are strongly correlated with this tree structure: items that are close in the tree tend to receive similar probabilities for any given user, making it difficult to distinguish among them based on user-specific preferences. We further show theoretically that such structural correlations prevent GR models from representing even simple patterns that can be well captured by conventional collaborative filtering models. To mitigate this issue, we propose Latte, a simple modification that injects a latent token before each semantic ID, reshaping the decoding space from a single tree into multiple latent-token-conditioned trees. This design creates multiple paths with varying tree distances between items, relaxing tree-induced probability coupling and yielding an average of 3.45% relative improvement on NDCG@10. Our code is available at https://github.com/hyp1231/Latte.

</details>


### [155] [Light-FMP: Lightweight Feature and Model Pruning for Enhanced Deep Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06441)
*Nghia Bui,Yue Ning,Lijing Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Light-FMP是一个轻量级框架，通过预训练、剪枝和继续训练三个阶段，使用硬具体分布识别重要特征并剪枝模型，在提升深度推荐系统效率的同时保持高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度推荐系统在处理高维输入特征时难以平衡计算效率和模型精度，现有方法要么侧重精度忽视训练效率，要么优先效率导致次优精度。

Method: 提出Light-FMP框架，包含三个阶段：1) 预训练阶段，使用硬具体分布在小规模数据子集上高效预训练掩码层以识别重要特征；2) 剪枝阶段，对模型和特征进行剪枝；3) 继续训练阶段，在剩余数据集上使用域自适应参数继续训练。

Result: 在真实推荐系统基准数据集上的实验表明，Light-FMP在效率和精度上均优于现有方法，同时保持可扩展性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Light-FMP通过轻量化的特征和模型剪枝策略，成功实现了深度推荐系统中计算效率与模型精度的有效平衡。

Abstract: Deep recommender systems (DRS) often face challenges in balancing computational efficiency and model accuracy, especially when handling high-dimensional input features. Existing methods either focus on improving accuracy while neglecting training efficiency or prioritize efficiency at the cost of suboptimal accuracy across tasks. We propose Light-FMP: Lightweight Feature and Model Pruning for Enhanced DRS, a lightweight framework that addresses the challenges through three key phases: \textit{pretraining}, \textit{pruning}, and \textit{continued training}. Using a hard concrete distribution, a masking layer is efficiently pretrained on a small data subset to identify important features. The model and features are then pruned, and training continues on the remaining dataset with domain-adapted parameters. Experiments on benchmark datasets from real-world recommender systems demonstrate that Light-FMP outperforms existing methods in both efficiency and accuracy while maintaining scalability and robustness.

</details>


### [156] [Superintelligent Retrieval Agent: The Next Frontier of Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06647)
*Zeyu Yang,Qi Ma,Jason Chen,Anshumali Shrivastava*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SIRA提出了一种面向检索的超级智能方法，通过将多轮探索性搜索压缩为单次语料区分性检索，利用LLM增强文档和查询的缺失词汇，结合文档频率统计进行术语过滤，最终通过单次加权BM25调用实现高效检索，在BEIR基准和问答任务上超越稠密检索和多轮智能体方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强智能体将检索视为黑箱，采用多轮探索式搜索，如同新手查询不熟悉数据库，而非领域专家基于先验知识的高效检索，导致不必要的检索轮次、高延迟和低召回率。

Method: SIRA定义检索超级智能为将多轮探索压缩为单次语料区分性检索。LLM离线增强文档缺失术语、预测查询遗漏的证据词汇，并通过文档频率统计工具调用过滤不存在的、过于常见或无法产生检索边际的候选术语，最终将原始查询与经过验证的扩展项结合进行单次加权BM25检索。

Result: 在十个BEIR基准和下游问答任务上，SIRA显著优于稠密检索器和最先进的多轮智能体基线，表明一次精心构造的词汇查询在LLM认知和轻量语料统计指导下，可以超过更昂贵的多轮搜索方法。

Conclusion: SIRA证明单次词汇检索在LLM引导和语料统计辅助下能够达到超越多轮搜索的性能，且保持可解释性、无需训练和高效率，为检索增强智能体提供了更优的范式。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented agents are increasingly the interface to large organizational knowledge bases, yet most still treat retrieval as a black box: they issue exploratory queries, inspect returned snippets, and iteratively reformulate until useful evidence emerges. This approach resembles how a newcomer searches an unfamiliar database rather than how an expert navigates it with strong priors about terminology and likely evidence, and results in unnecessary retrieval rounds, increased latency, and poor recall.
  We introduce \textit{SuperIntelligent Retrieval Agent} (SIRA), which defines \emph{superintelligence} in retrieval as the ability to compress multi-round exploratory search into a single corpus-discriminative retrieval action. SIRA does not merely ask what terms are relevant to the query; it asks which terms are likely to separate the desired evidence from corpus-level confusers. On the corpus side, an LLM enriches each document offline with missing search vocabulary; on the query side, it predicts evidence vocabulary omitted by the query; and document-frequency statistics as a tool call to filter proposed terms that are absent, overly common, or unlikely to create retrieval margin. The final retrieval step is a single weighted BM25 call combining the original query with the validated expansion.
  Across ten BEIR benchmarks and downstream question-answering tasks, SIRA achieves the significantly superior performance outperforming dense retrievers and state-of-the-art multi-round agentic baselines, demonstrating that one well-formed lexical query, guided by LLM cognition and lightweight corpus statistics, can exceed substantially more expensive multi-round search while remaining interpretable, training-free, and efficient.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [Nationwide EHR-Based Chronic Rhinosinusitis Prediction Using Demographic-Stratified Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05213)
*Sicong Chang,Yidan Shen,Justina Varghese,Akshay R Prabhakar,Sebastian Guadarrama-Sistos-Vazquez,Jiefu Chen,Masayoshi Takashima,Omar G. Ahmed,Renjie Hu,Xin Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用All of Us全国性纵向EHR数据，通过混合特征选择管道和人口统计学分层模型预测慢性鼻窦炎（CRS）诊断，在6个成人性别-生命阶段亚组中实现0.8461的AUC，比最佳基线提升0.0168，证明常规EHR数据可支持人群代表性的CRS风险分层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CRS是一种常见且异质性的炎症性疾病，早期识别困难，症状与变应性鼻炎等常见疾病重叠，且表型异质性进一步模糊了风险模式。现有预测研究多依赖单机构队列，缺乏人群层面的普适性。

Method: 利用All of Us研究项目的全国性纵向EHR数据，采用混合特征选择管道（结合基于患病率的统计筛选与基于模型的重要性排序），将约11万个候选代码压缩为100个可解释特征；针对6个成人性别-生命阶段亚组训练人口统计学分层模型并进行亚组特异性超参数调优。

Result: 整体AUC达到0.8461，比最佳基线提升0.0168。

Conclusion: 常规收集的EHR数据可支持具有人群代表性的CRS风险分层，有助于在初级保健中实现更早的分诊和转诊优先级排序。

Abstract: Chronic rhinosinusitis (CRS) is a common heterogeneous inflammatory disorder that causes substantial morbidity and healthcare costs. CRS is difficult to identify early from routine encounters, as symptom presentations overlap with common conditions such as allergic rhinitis, and heterogeneous phenotypes further obscure risk patterns. Prior predictive studies often rely on single-institutional cohorts , which reduce population-level generalizability. To overcome this, we leveraged nationwide longitudinal EHR data from the \textit{All of Us} Research Program to predict CRS diagnosis using two years of pre-diagnostic history. To address extreme feature sparsity and dimensionality in coded EHR data, we implemented a hybrid feature-selection pipeline that combines prevalence-based statistical screening with model-based importance ranking, compressing approximately 110,000 candidate codes into 100 interpretable features. To capture demographic heterogeneity, we trained demographic stratified models across six adult sex and life-stage subgroups with subgroup-specific hyperparameter tuning. Our framework achieved an overall AUC of 0.8461, improving discrimination by 0.0168 over the best baseline. These results demonstrate that routinely collected EHR data may support population-representative CRS risk stratification and inform earlier triage and referral prioritization in primary care.

</details>


### [158] [SAT: Sequential Agent Tuning for Coordinator Free Plug and Play Multi-LLM Training with Monotonic Improvement Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05216)
*Yi Xie,Yangyang Xu,Yi Fan,Bo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Sequential Agent Tuning (SAT)，一种无需协调器的多智能体训练范式，通过分块坐标更新和逐智能体KL信任域实现单调改进和即插即用，使3个4B智能体团队（总计12B）超越Qwen3-32B模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型参数多、部署成本高，而现有的多智能体协作方法在联合更新时面临复合分布偏移和训练不稳定的问题。

Method: 采用分解策略表示团队策略，通过分块坐标更新实现去中心化训练；提出序列感知的on-policy优势估计器，配合逐智能体KL信任域来隔离占据漂移；理论保证单调改进和即插即用不变性。

Result: 3个4B智能体（总计12B参数）在AIME24/25基准上平均超越Qwen3-32B达3.9%；将其中两个智能体替换为8B版本后，性能提升10.4%。

Conclusion: SAT框架不仅实现了多智能体团队的稳定高效训练，还支持智能体的即插即用升级，为低成本部署高性能LLM系统提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) with a large number of parameters achieve strong performance but are often prohibitively expensive to deploy. Recent work explores using teams of smaller, more efficient LLMs that collectively match or even outperform a single large model. However, jointly updating multiple agents introduces compounding distribution shifts, making coordination and stability during training difficult. We address this by introducing Sequential Agent Tuning (SAT), a coordinator-free training paradigm. SAT represents the team as a factorized policy and employs block-coordinate updates over agents, enabling scalable, decentralized training without a central controller. Specifically, we develop a sequence-aware, on-policy advantage estimator that conditions on the evolving team policy, coupled with per-agent KL trust regions that isolate occupancy drift. Theoretically, this framework provides two critical guarantees. First, it ensures monotonic improvement, stabilizing the training process. Second, it establishes provable plug-and-play invariance: any agent can be upgraded to a stronger model without retraining the rest of the team, with a formal guarantee that the performance bound improves. Empirically, a team of three 4B agents (12B total) trained with SAT surpasses the much larger Qwen3-32B on AIME24/25 benchmarks by 3.9\% on average. We validate our plug-and-play theory by swapping in two 8B agents, which boosts the composite score by 10.4\%. We provide code and appendix of proof at https://github.com/Yydc/SAT-AAMAS

</details>


### [159] [Horizon-Constrained Rashomon Sets for Chaotic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05218)
*Gauri Kale,Rahul Vishwakarma,Holly Diamond,Ava Hedayatipour,Amin Rezaei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将混沌理论与预测多重性联系起来，通过引入视界约束Rashomon集理论框架，揭示了混沌系统中模型多样性随预测时间演化的规律。实验表明该方法在保持预测性能的同时将决策质量提升18-34%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测多重性（Rashomon效应）与混沌动力学是机器学习中两个独立发展的基本挑战，尽管它们在概念上存在内在联系——混沌系统的指数发散特性可能导致近优模型集的动态变化，但缺乏统一的理论框架来桥接这两个领域。

Method: 提出视界约束Rashomon集理论框架，证明有效Rashomon集以最大李雅普诺夫指数决定的速率随提前时间指数收缩；引入李雅普诺夫加权度量以提供预测分歧的更紧界；开发基于决策对齐的模型选择算法，依据下游效用而非单纯预测精度进行模型筛选。

Result: 在Lorenz-96、Kuramoto-Sivashinsky等合成混沌系统以及风电、交通、天气等实际应用中，该方法在保持竞争性预测性能的同时将决策质量提升18-34%。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了混沌理论与预测多重性之间的首个严谨联系，为在安全关键的混沌领域部署机器学习提供了原则性指导。

Abstract: Predictive multiplicity and chaotic dynamics represent two fundamental challenges in machine learning that have evolved independently despite their conceptual connections. We bridge this gap by introducing horizon-constrained Rashomon sets, a theoretical framework that characterizes how model multiplicity evolves with prediction horizon in chaotic systems. Unlike static prediction tasks where the Rashomon set remains fixed, chaos induces exponential divergence among initially similar models, fundamentally transforming the nature of predictive equivalence. We prove that the effective Rashomon set contracts exponentially with lead time at a rate determined by the maximum Lyapunov exponent and introduce Lyapunov-weighted metrics that provide tighter bounds on predictive disagreement. Leveraging these insights, we develop decision-aligned selection algorithms that choose among near-optimal models based on downstream utility rather than forecast accuracy alone. Extensive experiments on synthetic chaotic systems (Lorenz-96, Kuramoto-Sivashinsky) and real-world applications (wind power, traffic, weather) demonstrate that our framework improves decision quality by 18-34\% while maintaining competitive predictive performance. This work establishes the first rigorous connection between chaos theory and predictive multiplicity, providing principled guidance for deploying machine learning in safety-critical chaotic domains.

</details>


### [160] [MidSteer: Optimal Affine Framework for Steering Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05220)
*Tatiana Gaintseva,Andrew Stepanov,Ziquan Liu,Martin Benning,Gregory Slabaugh,Jiankang Deng,Ismail Elezi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为概念引导（concept steering）建立了理论框架，证明了标准引导方法是LEACE（仿射概念擦除）的特例，提出了最优仿射解决方案LEACE-Switch，并进一步推出了更通用的MidSteer框架，实现了最小干扰的概念操控，在多种模型和任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念引导在生成模型的后部署对齐和安全设置中取得了实证成功，但缺乏全面的理论框架，本文旨在弥补这一理论空白。

Method: 首先建立引导与仿射概念擦除（LEACE）之间的理论联系；然后形式化概念切换的理论框架LEACE-Switch并刻画其最优性假设；在此基础上提出更通用的MidSteer框架，放松假设条件，实现有向的最小干扰变换。

Result: MidSteer在多种任务、模态和架构（包括视觉扩散模型和大语言模型）上均表现良好。

Conclusion: 本文为概念引导提供了统一的理论基础，并通过MidSteer实现了更灵活、更通用的概念操控方法。

Abstract: Steering intermediate representations has emerged as a powerful strategy for controlling generative models, particularly in post-deployment alignment and safety settings. However, despite its empirical success, it currently lacks a comprehensive theoretical framework. In this paper, we bridge this gap by formalizing the theory of concept steering. First, we establish a link between steering and affine concept erasure, proving that the standard approach for removing unwanted behaviors is a special case of LEACE (a closed-form method for affine erasure). Next, we formulate a principled theoretical framework for concept switching, LEACE-Switch, and characterize the assumptions under which it provides an optimal affine solution. Building on this analysis, we then introduce MidSteer (Minimal Disturbance concept Steering), a more general affine framework for concept manipulation that relaxes these assumptions and enables directed, minimal-disturbance transformations. We demonstrate that MidSteer performs favorably across a range of tasks, modalities, and architectures, including vision diffusion models and large language models.

</details>


### [161] [Adaptive Computation Depth via Learned Token Routing in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05222)
*Ahmed Abdelmuniem Abdalla Mohammed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Token-Selective Attention (TSA)，一种在连续Transformer块之间的残差更新上学习每个token门控的方法。门控是轻量级两层MLP，产生连续停止概率，实现端到端可微，参数开销仅1.7%。TSA学习与难度成比例的路由，即使无深度正则化也能跳过20%的token-层操作。在字符级语言建模上，TSA节省14-23%的计算量，质量损失<0.5%，且学习到的路由可直接用于推理时的稀疏执行以加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer对所有token应用相同数量的层，忽略了不同token的上下文难度差异，导致计算效率低下。

Method: 在连续Transformer块之间的残差更新上插入轻量级两层MLP门控，为每个token生成连续停止概率，实现端到端可微训练，无需修改基础架构。门控通过任务损失梯度自然学会跳过简单token的后续层计算。

Result: 在Tiny-Shakespeare和enwik8上节省14-23%的token-层操作，质量损失<0.5%；在相同效率下，验证损失比early exit低0.7%；学习路由可直接用于推理时稀疏执行实现实际加速。

Conclusion: TSA通过轻量级可微门控机制实现了与token难度成比例的自适应计算分配，在几乎不损失质量的情况下显著节省计算量，且无需架构修改即可直接获得推理加速。

Abstract: Standard transformer architectures apply the same number of layers to every token regardless of contextual difficulty. We present Token-Selective Attention (TSA), a learned per-token gate on residual updates between consecutive transformer blocks. Each gate is a lightweight two-layer multi-layer perceptron (MLP) that produces a continuous halting probability, making the mechanism end-to-end differentiable with 1.7% parameter overhead and no changes to the base architecture. Notably, TSA learns difficulty-proportional routing without any explicit depth pressure: even at $λ=0$ (no depth regularisation), the task-loss gradient alone drives the router to skip 20% of token-layer operations. On character-level language modeling, TSA saved 14-23% of token-layer operations (TLOps) across Tiny-Shakespeare and enwik8 at <0.5% quality loss. At matched efficiency, TSA achieved 0.7% lower validation loss than early exit, and the learned routing transfers directly to inference-time sparse execution for real wall-clock speedup.

</details>


### [162] [Structural Instability of Feature Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05223)
*Yunpeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出几何框架分析稀疏自编码器中特征组合的不稳定性，揭示了在高维稀疏锥流形下组合引导存在塌缩阈值，ReLU整流效应会导致类似棘轮效应的干扰累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器在解耦特征叠加和激活引导方面表现出色，但组合引导（同时激活多个语义潜变量）的理论基础尚不明确，现有线性表示假说忽略了过完备字典中的非线性干扰效应。

Method: 将激活空间建模为高维稀疏锥流形，利用球面字典模型推导组合塌缩阈值（由信号锥的高斯均宽/统计维度表征），分析高偏置下ReLU整流将微观方差波动转化为系统漂移的棘轮效应。

Result: 在CLEVR数据集上验证了预测的缩放趋势，层次化相关性加速了相较于随机基线的塌缩过渡。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了基于组合的引导在可扩展性上存在几何约束，需要设计显式管理干扰的组合机制，而非简单依赖线性叠加。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for disentangling feature superposition in transformer-based architectures, enabling precise control via activation steering. However, the theoretical foundations of compositional steering -- the simultaneous activation of distinct semantic latents -- remain under-explored. The prevailing Linear Representation Hypothesis often abstracts away non-linear interference effects that arise in overcomplete dictionaries. We present a geometric framework for analyzing the instability of feature unions. Modeling the activation space as a high-dimensional sparse cone manifold, we derive an asymptotic compositional-collapse threshold under a spherical dictionary model, characterized by the Gaussian mean width (statistical dimension) of the signal cone. We further show that, in the high-bias regime, ReLU rectification converts microscopic correlation-induced variance fluctuations into a systematic drift that accumulates under composition, yielding interference growth consistent with a ratchet effect. We validate the predicted scaling trends on structured semantic features extracted from CLEVR, where hierarchical correlations accelerate the transition relative to random baselines. Together, our results highlight geometric constraints on the scalability of union-based steering and motivate composition mechanisms that explicitly manage interference beyond naive linear superposition.

</details>


### [163] [Channel-Level Semantic Perturbations: Unlearnable Examples for Diverse Training Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05224)
*Bo Wang,Jia Ni,Mengnan Zhao,Zhan Qin,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了不可学习示例在预训练-微调范式下的表现，发现冻结预训练权重会显著削弱现有UE方法的效果，并提出了一种层级欺骗策略SSC来应对这一挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UE方法主要在从头训练设置下评估，未探究其在广泛采用的预训练-微调范式下的行为，存在关键研究空白。

Method: 提出层级欺骗策略SSC，将生成过程限制在语义有效子空间内，以绕过预训练权重引入的语义抑制。

Result: SSC方法在浅层冻结和语义聚焦预训练等挑战性范式下能持续保持数据不可学习性。

Conclusion: UE在PF范式下失效的原因是语义过滤机制，而SSC通过语义伪装有效桥接了基于预训练的不可学习学习的关键差距。

Abstract: The unauthorized use of personal data in model training has emerged as a growing privacy threat. Unlearnable examples (UEs) address this issue by embedding imperceptible perturbations into benign examples to obstruct feature learning. However, existing studies mainly evaluate UEs under from-scratch training settings, leaving their behavior under the widely adopted pretraining-finetuning (PF) paradigm largely unexplored. In this work, we provide the first systematic investigation of unlearnable examples across diverse training paradigms. Our analysis reveals that loading and freezing pretrained weights significantly weakens the effectiveness of existing UEs methods. We further explain these findings through semantic filtering: while UEs tend to induce models to overfit non-semantic noise, thereby weakening their semantic extraction capabilities, under the PF paradigm, frozen shallow layers preserve data semantics, effectively filtering out distracting information like unlearnable noise. Guided by these insights, we propose a hierarchical deception strategy, Shallow Semantic Camouflage (SSC), that confines the generation process to a semantically valid subspace, aiming to bypass the semantic suppression introduced by pretrained weights. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently preserves data unlearnability even under challenging training paradigms, such as shallow-layer freezing and semantic-focused pretraining (SF-Pretrain), bridging the critical gap in pretrain-based unlearnable learning.

</details>


### [164] [MACS: Modality-Aware Capacity Scaling for Efficient Multimodal MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05225)
*Bo Li,Chuan Wu,shaolin Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MACS是一个训练无关的推理框架，通过熵加权负载机制和动态模态自适应容量机制，解决MoE多模态大语言模型在专家并行推理中因信息异质性和模态动态性导致的效率瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE MLLMs在专家并行推理中受落后节点效应影响存在严重效率瓶颈，而现有基于token计数的负载均衡方法无法应对多模态场景下的两个独特挑战：(1)信息异质性——大量冗余视觉token与语义关键token被同等对待；(2)模态动态性——不同任务中视觉与文本比例变化导致资源错配。

Method: 提出MACS框架，包含两个核心机制：(1)熵加权负载机制——通过量化视觉token的语义价值来应对信息异质性；(2)动态模态自适应容量机制——根据输入实时的模态组成分配专家资源。

Result: 在多种多模态基准测试上，MACS显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: MACS为MoE MLLMs在EP推理中的高效部署提供了一种新颖且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts Multimodal Large Language Models (MoE MLLMs) suffer from a significant efficiency bottleneck during Expert Parallelism (EP) inference due to the straggler effect. This issue is worsened in the multimodal context, as existing token-count-based load balancing methods fail to address two unique challenges: (1) Information Heterogeneity, where numerous redundant visual tokens are treated equally to semantically critical ones, and (2) Modality Dynamics, where varying visual to text ratios across tasks lead to resource misallocation. To address these challenges, we propose MACS (Modality-Aware Capacity Scaling), a training-free inference framework. Specifically, MACS introduces an Entropy-Weighted Load mechanism to quantify the semantic value of visual tokens, addressing information heterogeneity. Additionally, the Dynamic Modality-Adaptive Capacity mechanism allocates expert resources based on the real-time modal composition of the input. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MACS significantly outperforms existing methods on various multimodal benchmarks, providing a novel and robust solution for the efficient deployment of MoE MLLMs in EP inference.

</details>


### [165] [Internalizing Outcome Supervision into Process Supervision: A New Paradigm for Reinforcement Learning for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05226)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng,Sibo wang,Huiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将推理任务中的强化学习问题重新概念化为"将结果监督内化为过程监督"，并据此设计了一种监督内化方法，使模型能够从失败的推理轨迹中自动提取过程级学习信号，实现细粒度策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习用于推理面临核心挑战：结果级监督稀疏导致信用分配困难，而外部过程监督成本高昂且难以可持续扩展。

Method: 提出监督内化方法，使模型在强化学习过程中自动从失败推理轨迹中识别、纠正和重用错误步骤，从而提取过程级学习信号；进一步将其抽象为一种新的训练范式，让模型在强化学习期间持续生成并优化自身内部的过程监督。

Result: 在仅有结果监督的条件下实现了更细粒度的策略优化，能够自动提取过程级学习信号，为细粒度信用分配开辟了新路径。

Conclusion: 推理强化学习的本质可以理解为将结果监督内化为过程监督的问题，所提方法在不依赖外部标注的情况下实现了内部过程监督的自动生成与优化，提供了一种区别于传统外部过程监督的新范式。

Abstract: The central challenge of reinforcement learning for reasoning lies not only in the sparsity of outcome-level supervision, but more fundamentally in how to transform feedback provided only at the end of a sequence into fine-grained learning signals that can guide intermediate reasoning steps. Existing approaches either rely on outcome-level rewards for sequence-level optimization, which makes precise credit assignment difficult, or depend on externally constructed process supervision, which is costly and difficult to scale sustainably. To address this, we propose a new perspective: reinforcement learning for reasoning can be understood as the problem of internalizing outcome supervision into process supervision. From this perspective, we introduce a supervision-internalization method for reinforcement learning for reasoning, enabling the model to automatically extract process-level learning signals through identifying, correcting, and reusing failed reasoning trajectories, thereby achieving finer-grained policy optimization under outcome-only supervision. We further abstract this idea into a new training paradigm, in which the model continually generates and refines its own internal process supervision during reinforcement learning, opening a new path for fine-grained credit assignment in reinforcement learning for reasoning that differs from externally provided process supervision.

</details>


### [166] [Rethinking Data Curation in LLM Training: Online Reweighting Offers Better Generalization than Offline Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05227)
*Wanru Zhao,Yihong Chen,Yuzhi Tang,Wentao Ma,Shengchao Hu,Shell Xu Hu,Alex Iacob,Abhinav Mehrotra,Nicholas D. Lane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ADAPT是一种在线数据重加权框架，通过自适应逐样本学习率动态调整训练样本重要性，替代传统离线数据筛选/混合方法，在指令微调和大规模预训练中显著提升跨基准泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM训练中的数据策展方法（如数据筛选、混合）采用离线范式，与训练过程脱节，导致：（1）模型/任务变化时需重新执行整个流程，工程开销大；（2）通过硬过滤或重采样改变数据规模，牺牲数据多样性，损害泛化能力。

Method: 将数据策展重新定义为在线重加权问题，提出ADAPT框架：在训练过程中通过基于相似性的质量信号动态调整逐样本学习率（而非静态预处理），不改变训练样本数量。作为隐式课程学习者，随模型演化逐步从粗粒度模式聚焦到细粒度语义区分。

Result: 在指令微调和大规模预训练实验中，ADAPT在等FLOPs条件下持续优于离线筛选/混合方法及现有在线方法，实现更强的跨基准泛化能力。

Conclusion: 在线动态重加权范式相比传统离线策展更有效，ADAPT通过自适应学习率机制在不牺牲多样性的前提下提升训练效率和模型泛化性能。

Abstract: Data curation is a critical yet under-explored area in large language model (LLM) training. Existing methods, such as data selection and mixing, operate in an offline paradigm, detaching themselves from training. This separation introduces engineering overhead and makes the curation brittle: the entire pipeline must be re-run under model/task shifts. Moreover, offline methods alter data size through hard filtering or resampling, often sacrificing data diversity and harming generalization. We propose to rethink data curation as an online reweighting problem, where sample importance is dynamically adjusted during training via loss weighting rather than static pre-processing. Specifically, we introduce ADAPT (Adaptive Data reweighting for Pretraining and FineTuning), a dynamic online framework that reweights training samples with adaptive per-sample learning rates guided by similarity-based quality signals, without changing the number of training samples. Unlike offline methods that enforce a static data distribution, ADAPT acts as an implicit curriculum learner, progressively shifting focus from coarse-grained patterns to fine-grained semantic distinctions as the model evolves. Experiments on both instruction tuning and large-scale pretraining show that ADAPT consistently outperforms offline selection/mixing and prior online methods, achieving stronger cross-benchmark generalization under equal FLOPs.

</details>


### [167] [Attribution-Guided Continual Learning for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05285)
*Yazheng Liu,Yuxuan Wan,Rui Xu,Xi Zhang,Sihong Xie,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于归因引导的持续微调框架，通过逐元素评估Transformer层中参数对特定任务的重要性，动态调整梯度更新幅度，在持续学习中缓解灾难性遗忘，在保留旧任务知识的同时学习新任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习方法（数据重放、参数冻结、正则化）缺乏对LLMs内部知识分布的语义感知能力，无法区分哪些参数应保留或更新，导致缓解灾难性遗忘的效果有限。

Method: 提出归因引导的持续微调框架：首先估算每个Transformer层中参数对特定任务的逐元素重要性分数，然后利用这些分数调制梯度——对先前任务重要的参数更新幅度小，不重要的参数保持可塑性以学习新任务。

Result: 在持续学习基准测试中，该方法在旧任务知识保留和新任务学习性能上均持续优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过归因引导的参数重要性评估与梯度调制，可有效平衡LLM在持续学习中的稳定性与可塑性，缓解灾难性遗忘。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often suffer from catastrophic forgetting in continual learning: after learning new tasks sequentially, they perform worse on earlier tasks. Existing methods mitigate catastrophic forgetting by data replay, parameter freezing, or regularization. However, these methods lack semantic awareness of internal knowledge distribution in LLMs. As a result, they cannot distinguish parameters that should be preserved or updated. We propose an attribution-guided continual fine-tuning framework for LLMs. Our method estimates task-specific, element-wise parameter importance in each Transformer layer and uses these scores to modulate gradients. Parameters important to previous tasks receive smaller updates, while less relevant ones remain plastic for learning new tasks. Experiments on continual learning benchmarks show that our method consistently outperforms baselines, achieving better retention of old tasks while maintaining competitive performance on new tasks.

</details>


### [168] [COPYCOP: Ownership Verification for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05360)
*Rahul Nandakumar,Deepayan Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CopyCop是一种能够识别抄袭GNN的算法，即便对方使用了不同的架构、权重、嵌入维度或进行了嵌入变换，也能准确检测出抄袭关系，并提供理论保证和实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的水印和指纹方法无法在攻击者变换嵌入、使用不同架构或维度的条件下有效识别抄袭GNN，因此需要一种新的检测方法。

Method: 提出CopyCop算法，通过分析两个GNN输出的节点嵌入之间的关系来判定是否存在抄袭，无需依赖水印或指纹，具有理论保证。

Result: 在14个数据集和5种GNN架构上的实验表明，CopyCop准确且鲁棒，能抵抗多种对抗攻击和嵌入变换。

Conclusion: CopyCop能有效识别经过刻意隐藏的抄袭GNN，填补了现有方法在严格条件下的检测空白。

Abstract: Given two GNNs that output node embeddings, how can we determine if they were trained independently? An adversary could have trained one GNN specifically to mimic the other GNN's embeddings. To obscure this relationship between the GNNs, the adversarial GNN might then transform its output embeddings. The two GNNs could have different architectures, weights, and embedding dimensions, and the adversary can transform the embeddings. Despite these stringent conditions, our algorithm (named CopyCop) can identify such copycat GNNs, unlike existing watermarking and fingerprinting methods. We also provide theoretical guarantees for CopyCop. Finally, experiments on 14 datasets and 5 GNN architectures demonstrate that CopyCop is accurate and robust against a broad class of adversarial attacks and transformations. Code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/CopyCop-Graph-Ownership-Verification-8143/README.md

</details>


### [169] [SPADE: Faster Drug Discovery by Learning from Sparse Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05370)
*Rahul Nandakumar,Ben Fauber,Deepayan Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPADE是一种新型配体选择算法，仅需平均40次测试即可找到10个高质量配体，在样本效率上比深度学习和贝叶斯优化方法提升7%-32%，且速度比最接近的竞争者快10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物发现中候选配体通过率极低（<5%），且需要对无先验数据的新型蛋白质进行高效配体筛选，以减少测试次数并提高成功率。

Method: 提出SPADE算法，采用新颖的配体选择策略，通过学习稀疏数据进行高效筛选。

Result: 平均仅需40次测试即可找到10个高质量配体；在一对一比较中，SPADE在更多蛋白质上优于深度学习和贝叶斯优化方法，样本效率中位数提升7%-32%；评分速度比最接近的竞争者快10倍。

Conclusion: SPADE在样本效率和计算速度上均显著优于现有方法，为无先验数据的新型蛋白质药物发现提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Drug discovery seeks molecules (ligands) that bind strongly and selectively to a target protein. However, fewer than 5% of candidate ligands pass the bar for even the early stages of drug discovery. Furthermore, we want methods that work for novel proteins for which we have no prior data. Starting from scratch, we have to iteratively select and test candidate ligands such that we find enough ligands of the desired quality in as few tests as possible. Our proposed algorithm, named SPADE, introduces a novel approach to ligand selection that requires only 40 tests on average to find 10 high-quality ligands. In one-vs-one comparisons, SPADE outperforms deep learning and Bayesian optimization methods on more proteins, achieving median improvements of 7%-32% in sample efficiency. SPADE is also 10x faster than its closest competitor at scoring candidate drugs. Dataset and code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SPADE_Fast_Drug_Discovery_by_Learning_from_Sparse_Data-F028/README.md

</details>


### [170] [Two-Stage Learned Decomposition for Scalable Routing on Multigraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05389)
*Filip Rydin,Morteza Haghir Chehreghani,Balázs Kulcsár*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种节点-边策略分解（NEPF）方法，通过将路由策略分解为节点排列和边选择两个阶段，并引入预编码边聚合方案、非自回归架构以及分层强化学习，有效解决了多图车辆路径问题中的可扩展性难题，在六个VRP变体上实现了与最先进方法相当或更优的解质量，同时显著加快了训练和推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经方法主要限于欧几里得设置或简单图，而多图（平行边代表不同旅行选项）场景下的方法面临严重的可扩展性问题，亟需一种高效且可扩展的解决方案。

Method: 提出NEPF方法，将路由策略分解为节点排列阶段和边选择阶段；引入预编码边聚合方案和非自回归架构用于边阶段，并使用分层强化学习方法联合训练两个阶段。

Result: 在六个VRP变体上，NEPF在解质量上匹配或超越最先进方法，同时训练和推理速度显著更快。

Conclusion: NEPF有效解决了多图VRP的可扩展性问题，为更复杂的路由问题提供了一种高效且高质量的解决方案。

Abstract: Most neural methods for Vehicle Routing Problems (VRPs) are limited to Euclidean settings or simple graphs. In this work, we instead consider multigraphs, where parallel edges represent distinct travel options with varying trade-offs (e.g., distance vs time). Few methods are designed for such formulations and those that do exist face major scalability issues. We mitigate these scalability issues via a Node-Edge Policy Factorization (NEPF) approach, which splits the routing policy into a node permutation stage and an edge selection stage. To enable the decomposition, we introduce a pre-encoding edge aggregation scheme and a non-autoregressive architecture for the edge stage, as well as a hierarchical reinforcement learning method to train the stages jointly. Our experiments across six VRP variants demonstrate that NEPF matches or outperforms the state-of-the-art in terms of solution quality, while being significantly faster in training and inference.

</details>


### [171] [Differentiable Parameter Optimization for DAEs with State-Dependent Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05395)
*Ion Matei,Maksym Zhenirovskyy,Anthony Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对具有状态事件的半显式微分代数方程（DAE）系统，本文提出了两种互补的梯度计算方法：自动微分-通过-仿真方法和显式离散伴随方法，用于解决含代数变量、事件时间和重置映射的参数学习问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含状态事件的DAE系统（如受代数方程约束且存在模式切换、冲击或状态重新初始化的系统）中，梯度计算面临代数变量隐式定义、事件时间依赖参数、重置映射引入不连续性等挑战，现有方法难以处理此类约束优化问题。

Method: 1. 将学习问题建模为含DAE动力学、代数约束、守卫方程和重置映射的约束最小二乘问题；2. 提出自动微分-通过-仿真方法：在向量场内部求解代数变量，利用隐函数定理对代数求解进行微分，并通过分段可微积分处理事件；3. 提出显式离散伴随方法：将前向仿真表示为事件分割的残差系统，通过求解光滑段和事件残差的拉格朗日乘子计算梯度。

Result: 两种方法均能为前向仿真所选择的事件路径提供梯度，在固定事件顺序和横截守卫穿越条件下有效；明确了伴随方法中的残差项是等式约束而非启发式惩罚项。

Conclusion: 从梯度解释、事件时间处理、实现复杂性和局部有效性四个方面对两种方法进行了比较，为含事件DAE系统的可微参数优化提供了理论基础和实用方案。

Abstract: Differential-algebraic equations (DAEs) with state-dependent events arise in systems whose continuous dynamics are constrained by algebraic equations and interrupted by mode changes, switching logic, impacts, or state reinitializations. Gradient-based parameter learning for such systems is challenging because algebraic variables are implicitly defined, event times depend on the parameters, and reset maps introduce discontinuities. This paper studies differentiable parameter optimization for semi-explicit DAEs with events. We formulate the learning problem as a constrained least-squares problem with DAE dynamics, algebraic constraints, guard equations, and reset maps. We then develop two complementary gradient-computation strategies. The first is an automatic-differentiation-through-simulation method that solves algebraic variables inside the vector field, differentiates the algebraic solve using the implicit function theorem, and handles events through segmented differentiable integration. The second is an explicit discrete-adjoint method that represents the forward simulation as an event-split residual system and computes gradients by solving for the Lagrange multipliers of smooth-segment and event residuals. The formulation clarifies that residual terms in the adjoint method are equality constraints, not heuristic penalties. We compare the two approaches in terms of gradient interpretation, event-time handling, implementation complexity, and local validity. Both methods provide gradients for the event path selected by the forward simulation and are valid under fixed event ordering and transversal guard crossings.

</details>


### [172] [Information Theoretic Adversarial Training of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05415)
*Yiwei Zhang,Jeremiah Birrell,Reza Ebrahimi,Rouzbeh Behnia,Jason Pacheco,Elisa Bertino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WARDEN 提出了一种分布鲁棒的对抗训练框架，通过 f-散度模糊集对对抗样本进行动态重加权，在降低攻击成功率的同时保持模型效用，计算成本与现有方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM对抗训练方法（如CAT、CAPO）计算成本高且难以扩展，需要更高效的鲁棒对齐方法。

Method: 基于分布鲁棒优化，通过 f-散度（KL散度）模糊集围绕经验训练分布构建，利用对偶形式将目标转化为 log-sum-exp 形式，通过动态参数控制重加权强度，自动强调更难对抗样本。

Result: 在多个LLM和攻击设置下，WARDEN显著降低了攻击成功率，计算和效用成本与CAT、CAPO、MixAT基线相当。

Conclusion: WARDEN 提供了一种计算可行且可扩展的鲁棒对齐方法，在分布鲁棒框架下实现对抗训练的高效重加权。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to adversarial prompting despite advances in alignment and safety, often exhibiting harmful behaviors under novel attack strategies. While adversarial training can improve robustness, existing approaches are computationally expensive and difficult to scale. Recent continuous adversarial training methods, such as Continuous adversarial training (CAT) and Continuous Adversarial Preference Optimization (CAPO), address this challenge by leveraging gradient-based perturbations in the embedding space, enabling more efficient and expressive attacks. Building on this paradigm, we propose WARDEN, a distributionally robust adversarial training framework for LLMs that dynamically reweights adversarial examples through an f -divergence ambiguity set around the empirical training distribution. Our method optimizes the worst-case adversarial loss within a divergence ball around the empirical data distribution, automatically emphasizing harder adversarial examples. Using the convex dual formulation, the objective reduces to a log-sum-exp form under the KL divergence, with a dynamical parameter controlling the strength of reweighting. This study leads to a new class of information-theoretic objectives that significantly reduce attack success rates while maintaining model utility. Across multiple LLMs and attack settings, WARDEN substantially reduces attack success rates with computational and utility costs comparable to CAT-, CAPO-, and MixAT-based baselines, making it a practical approach for scalable robust alignment.

</details>


### [173] [Active Learning for Conditional Generative Compressed Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05435)
*Alexander DeLise,Nick Dexter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于提示条件生成模型的压缩感知问题，将提示分为设计采样分布和定义恢复模型两个角色，理论上证明了提示匹配的Christoffel采样可保持近最优的恢复界，而提示不匹配会带来兼容性惩罚，实验表明提示显著影响采样分布和图像恢复效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成压缩感知方法使用预训练生成器的值域作为非线性模型，但缺乏对条件版本（prompt-conditioned）的系统理论研究，尤其是提示在采样和恢复中的不同作用尚未被区分和分析。

Method: 将提示条件化分为两个角色：设计采样分布的提示和定义恢复模型的提示。针对ReLU和Lipschitz条件生成器推导稳定恢复界，分析提示匹配与不匹配两种情况的理论保证。使用Stable Diffusion进行实验验证。

Result: 提示匹配的Christoffel采样保持了与现存近最优生成压缩感知理论相同的Christoffel复杂度常数；提示不匹配则引入显式的兼容性惩罚。实验表明提示能有效重塑Christoffel采样分布并影响图像恢复质量。

Conclusion: 提示应被视为具有不同影响（感知、近似、恢复）的设计变量，为提示条件化生成压缩感知提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Generative compressed sensing uses the range of a pretrained generator as a nonlinear model for recovering structured signals from limited measurements. We study a conditional version of this problem for image recovery from subsampled Fourier measurements using prompt-conditioned generative models. Our framework separates two roles of conditioning: the prompt used to design the sampling distribution and the prompt used to define the recovery model. For ReLU and Lipschitz conditional generators, we prove stable recovery bounds showing that prompt-matched Christoffel sampling retains the same Christoffel complexity constant as existing near-optimal generative compressed sensing theory, while prompt mismatch incurs an explicit compatibility penalty. Experiments with Stable Diffusion show that prompts meaningfully reshape Christoffel sampling distributions and influence image recovery. Overall, our results suggest that prompts should be treated as design variables with distinct effects on sensing, approximation, and recovery.

</details>


### [174] [On Semantic Loss Fine-Tuning Approach for Preventing Model Collapse in Causal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05438)
*Pratik Deshmukh,Atirek Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 标准微调Transformer模型进行因果推理会导致灾难性模型崩溃（100%崩溃率），模型学会总是预测"Yes"或"No"的平凡解。本文提出基于图的语义损失函数和动态lambda调度，在transitivity任务上达到70.4%准确率，在d-separation任务上达到68.6%准确率，相比崩溃基线提升42.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准微调Transformer模型进行因果推理任务时，模型会出现灾难性崩溃，学习到忽略输入结构的平凡解（如总是回答"Yes"或"No"），导致误导性的高准确率但实际未学到任何因果推理能力。

Method: 提出一种包含图结构逻辑约束和动态lambda调度的语义损失函数。具体包括：(1) 基于图的逻辑约束来强制模型关注输入结构；(2) 动态lambda调度机制来平衡损失项；(3) 在Gemma 270M模型上对transitivity和d-separation两个因果推理任务进行微调。

Result: 在transitivity任务上达到70.4%准确率，d-separation任务上达到68.6%准确率，相比崩溃基线提升42.7%。在1000个对抗性样本上，语义模型达到67-70%准确率，而崩溃模型仅为43-71%。在超过20万评估样本的5个模型变体上验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 语义损失对于Transformer实现稳定的因果推理是必要的而非可选的。该方法的图结构逻辑约束和动态调度机制能有效防止模型崩溃，使模型产生稳定的、依赖于上下文的预测。

Abstract: Standard fine-tuning of transformer models on causal reasoning tasks leads to catastrophic model collapse, where models learn trivial solutions such as always predicting "Yes" or "No" regardless of input structure. We demonstrate that fine-tuning Gemma 270M on transitivity and d-separation tasks without semantic loss results in 100% collapse rate, with models achieving misleadingly high accuracy (73.9%) while learning no causal reasoning. We propose a semantic loss function with graph-based logical constraints and dynamic lambda scheduling that prevents this collapse. Our approach achieves 70.4% accuracy on transitivity tasks and 68.6% on d-separation tasks with stable, context-dependent predictions, representing a 42.7% improvement over collapsed baselines. Adversarial evaluation on 1,000 structural reasoning samples shows semantic models achieve 67-70% accuracy while collapsed models fail catastrophically at 43-71%. We validate our findings through comprehensive benchmarking on 200,000+ evaluation samples across five model variants, demonstrating that semantic loss is essential and not optional, for stable causal reasoning in transformers.

</details>


### [175] [Robustness of Graph Self-Supervised Learning to Real-World Noise: A Case Study on Text-Driven Biomedical Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05463)
*Othmane Kabal,Mounira Harzallah,Fabrice Guillet,Hideaki Takeda,Ryutaro Ichise*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估了图自监督学习（GSSL）在文本驱动噪声图上的表现，提出了NATD-GSSL框架，发现不同 pretext 任务和 GNN 架构对噪声的鲁棒性存在显著差异，关系重建易受噪声影响而特征重建鲁棒性较强，双向关系消息传递架构更适合噪声图。该方法在无监督术语分类任务上相比预训练语言模型提升达7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GSSL方法假设图是干净且人工整理的，但NLP技术进步使得可以从文本中自动大规模提取知识图谱，这引入了现实世界中的噪声。此前关于GSSL鲁棒性的研究大多依赖合成扰动，未充分探索这种真实的文本驱动噪声，存在研究空白。

Method: 提出NATD-GSSL框架，包含自动图构建、图精炼和GSSL三个模块。采用双图评估协议，将来自MedMentions的噪声图与干净的UMLS参考图进行对比，并通过共享的金标准对齐。系统评估了不同 pretext 任务（关系重建、特征重建、对比学习）和不同GNN架构（双向关系消息传递、单向关系消息传递等）在噪声环境下的表现。

Result: 关系重建对噪声高度敏感但受益于良好定义的模式；特征重建对噪声鲁棒，性能接近干净图设置；对比学习受噪声影响较小但高度依赖与下游任务的对齐。双向关系消息传递GNN更适合噪声文本驱动图，单向关系GNN在干净图上表现最佳。整体相比预训练语言模型基线提升达7%。

Conclusion: NATD-GSSL为在真实世界噪声图上应用GSSL提供了实用指导。不同pretext任务和GNN架构对噪声的鲁棒性差异显著，需根据噪声特性选择合适的方法。特征重建和双向关系消息传递架构是处理噪声文本驱动图的优选策略。

Abstract: Graph Self-Supervised Learning (GSSL) offers a powerful paradigm for learning graph representations without labeled data. However, existing work assumes clean, manually curated graphs. Recent advances in NLP enable the large-scale automatic extraction of knowledge graphs from text, opening new opportunities for GSSL while introducing substantial real-world noise. This type of noise remains largely unexplored, as prior robustness studies typically rely on synthetic perturbations. To address this gap, we present the first comprehensive evaluation of GSSL methods on text-driven graphs for unsupervised term typing. We introduce Noise-Aware Text-Driven Graph GSSL (NATD-GSSL), a unified framework that combines automatic graph construction, graph refinement, and GSSL. Our evaluation follows a dual-graph protocol that contrasts a noisy graph derived from MedMentions with a clean Unified Medical Language System (UMLS) reference graph, aligned through a shared gold standard. Our results reveal variability in robustness across both pretext tasks and Graph Neural Network (GNN) architectures. Relation reconstruction is highly sensitive to noise and benefits from well-defined schemas, whereas feature reconstruction is considerably more robust, achieving performance comparable to clean-graph settings. Contrastive objectives are generally less affected by noise but depend strongly on alignment with downstream tasks. GNN architecture also plays a critical role: bidirectional relational message-passing designs are better suited to noisy, text-driven graphs, while unidirectional relational ones perform best on clean graphs. Overall, NATD-GSSL provides practical guidance for applying GSSL to real-world, noisy graphs and achieves up to a 7\% improvement over pretrained language model baselines. All code and benchmarks are publicly available at https://github.com/OthmaneKabal/MC2GAE.

</details>


### [176] [GRALIS: A Unified Canonical Framework for Linear Attribution Methods via Riesz Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05480)
*Raimondo Fanale*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRALIS 是一个统一的数学框架，基于 Riesz 表示定理为可解释 AI 归因方法建立了表示理论，将 SHAP、IG、LIME 和线性化 GradCAM 纳入同一形式体系，满足 13.5/14 条公理属性，并在 BreaKHis 数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流 XAI 归因方法（GradCAM、SHAP、LIME、Integrated Gradients）基于不同的理论基础，缺乏形式化的比较框架。作者旨在建立一个统一的数学框架，使这些方法在理论上可比并同时满足多种理想属性。

Method: 提出 GRALIS 框架，通过 Riesz 表示定理证明：L²(Q,μ) 上每个可加、线性、连续的归因泛函都有唯一规范表示 (Q, w, Δ)。该框架涵盖 SHAP、IG、LIME 和线性化 GradCAM，并提供了七条形式化定理保证。通过 Möbius 变换建立 GRALIS 与 Shapley 交互值的代数对应。

Result: 在 BreaKHis 数据集（1,187 张组织学图像，DenseNet-121）上初步验证：删除忠实度 AUC 提升 +0.015（恶性），96% 类条件一致性，SAL = 0.762±0.109，稀疏度指数 0.39。

Conclusion: GRALIS 在公理完备性（13.5/14 条）上显著优于单个方法（2.5-6/14 条），同时满足完整性、敏感性、局部性、阶-k 交互和最优多尺度聚合等属性。与基线方法的全面比较将在后续论文中展开。

Abstract: The main XAI attribution methods for deep neural networks -- GradCAM, SHAP, LIME, Integrated Gradients -- operate on separate theoretical foundations and are not formally comparable. We present GRALIS (Gradient-Riesz Averaged Locally-Integrated Shapley), a mathematical framework establishing a representation theory for attributions: every additive, linear, and continuous attribution functional on L^2(Q,mu) admits a unique canonical representation (Q, w, Delta), proved necessary by the Riesz Representation Theorem. This class encompasses SHAP, IG, LIME and linearized GradCAM, but excludes nonlinear functionals such as standard GradCAM or attention maps. Seven formal theorems provide simultaneous guarantees absent in any individual method: (T1) necessary canonical form; (T2) exact completeness; (T3) Monte Carlo convergence O(1/sqrt(m))+O(1/k); (T4) exact Shapley Interaction Values; (T5) Hoeffding ANOVA decomposition; (T6) Sobol sensitivity generalization; (T7) multi-scale extension (MS-GRALIS) with minimum-variance weights. An algebraic appendix justifies the GRALIS-SIV correspondence via the Mobius transform without circularity. GRALIS satisfies 13.5/14 axiomatic properties vs. 2.5-6/14 for individual methods, including completeness, sensitivity, locality, order-k interactions and optimal multi-scale aggregation simultaneously. Preliminary validation on BreaKHis (1,187 histology images, DenseNet-121) reports deletion faithfulness AUC +0.015 (malignant), 96% class-conditional consistency, SAL = 0.762+/-0.109 and sparsity index 0.39. Extended comparison with baseline XAI methods is planned for a companion paper.

</details>


### [177] [Approximate Next Policy Sampling: Replacing Conservative Target Policy Updates in Deep RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05481)
*Dillon Sandhu,Ronald Parr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出近似下一策略采样（ANPS）方法，通过修改训练数据分布而非约束策略更新来解决强化学习中的“先有鸡还是先有蛋”问题。在此基础上引入稳定值近似策略迭代（SV-API），其保持目标策略固定，由行为策略迭代收集相关经验，满足收敛标准后才提交新策略。将SV-API应用于PPO得到SV-PPO，在高维离散（Atari）和连续控制基准测试中匹配或提升性能，同时执行更大规模的目标策略更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决强化学习中策略安全改进的经典问题：策略更新后，价值函数需要在新策略的状态访问分布上准确，但该分布未知且无法采样用于训练价值函数。保守更新虽能解决但会缩小策略更新幅度。

Method: 提出ANPS（Approximate Next Policy Sampling）方法，通过修改训练数据分布使其近似下一策略的分布来解决该问题。具体实现为SV-API：在近似策略迭代循环中保持目标策略固定，由迭代更新的行为策略收集经验，满足收敛标准后才提交新策略。将其应用于PPO得到SV-PPO。

Result: SV-PPO在Atari高维离散控制和连续控制基准测试中匹配或提升了性能，同时能够执行更大规模的目标策略更新。满足稳定性标准时更新保证安全，否则不比标准近似策略迭代更差。

Conclusion: ANPS是解决RL中策略安全改进经典问题的一种可行且有效的新方案，通过改变训练分布而非约束更新幅度来实现安全策略改进。

Abstract: We revisit a classic "chicken-and-egg" problem in reinforcement learning: to safely improve a policy, the value function must be accurate on the state-visitation distribution of the updated policy. That distribution over states is unknown and cannot be sampled for the purposes of training the value function. Conservative updates solve this problem, but at the cost of shrinking the policy update. This paper explores an alternative solution, Approximate Next Policy Sampling (ANPS), which addresses the problem by modifying the training distribution rather than constraining the policy update. ANPS is satisfied if the distribution of the training data approximates that of the next policy. To demonstrate the feasibility and efficacy of ANPS, we introduce Stable Value Approximate Policy Iteration (SV-API). SV-API modifies the standard approximate policy iteration loop to hold the target policy fixed while an iteratively updated behavioral policy gathers relevant experience. It only commits to a new policy once a convergence criterion has been met. If certain stability criteria are met, the update is guaranteed to be safe; otherwise, it remains no less safe than standard approximate policy iteration. Applying SV-API to PPO yields Stable Value PPO (SV-PPO), which matches or improves performance on high-dimensional discrete (Atari) and continuous control benchmarks while executing substantially larger target policy updates. These results demonstrate the viability of ANPS as a new solution to this classic challenge in RL.

</details>


### [178] [MEMOA: Massive Mixtures of Online Agents via Mean-Field Decentralized Nash Equilibria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05492)
*Xuwei Yang,David B. Emerson,Fatemeh Tavakoli,Anastasis Kratsios*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种联邦学习中的分散式智能策略，通过闭式优化最小化最弱客户端的遗憾，并在大规模极限下证明该策略收敛于纳什最优的集中式策略，同时采用在线加权机制优化预测混合，实验验证了其性能优于贪婪分散基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在大规模场景下计算和通信成本难以随智能体数量扩展，而分散式策略中每个智能体仅依赖自身状态和群体均值场，亟需一种可扩展且最优的分散式策略。

Method: 1) 推导闭式最优分散式策略，以最小化最弱客户端的遗憾（最坏客户端/极小极大准则）；2) 证明大规模极限下分散策略收敛于纳什最优集中式策略；3) 通过在线加权机制优化服务器客户端预测的混合，以改善均值预测。

Result: 理论推导出闭式最优分散策略；证明其渐近收敛于集中式纳什最优策略；数值实验验证优于自然贪婪分散基线。

Conclusion: 分散式策略在大规模联邦学习中可同时实现可扩展性与最优性，在线加权机制进一步提升了整体预测性能。

Abstract: In the modern age of large-scale AI, federated learning has become an increasingly important tool for training large populations of AI agents; however, its computational and communication costs can rapidly fail to scale with the number of agents. This is precisely where decentralized agentic strategies shine: each agent acts autonomously, using only its own state together with a minimal summary of the ensemble, namely the mean-field. We derive the unique optimal decentralized policy in closed form. Optimality is characterized through a worst-client/minimax criterion: minimizing the under-performer regret, namely the maximal online cost incurred by the weakest agent in the ensemble. We further prove that the resulting decentralized policy asymptotically converges, in the large-population limit, to the Nash-optimal centralized policy, whose direct computation is not scalable. We use an online weighting mechanism to optimize the server-computed mixture of client predictions, thereby improving the mean prediction in addition to the previously optimized weakest-client prediction. Numerical experiments verify our theoretical guarantees and demonstrate that our decentralized policy typically outperforms natural greedy decentralized baselines.

</details>


### [179] [Shortcut Solutions Learned by Transformers Impair Continual Compositional Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05495)
*William T. Redman,Erik C. Johnson,Brian Robinson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文扩展了LEGO框架至持续学习（CL）场景，系统研究了前馈Transformer（BERT）和循环Transformer（ALBERT）在持续学习中的表现。发现BERT会学习捷径解，限制泛化和前向迁移；而ALBERT学习类似For循环的解决方案，CL性能更好。但在需要跨经验组合的CL任务中，两种模型均失败。ALBERT可通过混合训练数据挽救性能下降，BERT则因其捷径解而无法挽救。结果表明循环架构（ALBERT）具有更适合CL的归纳偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类的类比能力关键在于识别和利用跨领域的共同特征，这对持续学习至关重要。尽管Transformer在组合推理方面的研究很多，但很少有人系统研究它们如何利用已有表征学习新的相关经验。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将先前开发的LEGO框架扩展为持续学习（CL）版本（"continual LEGO"），在此范式下比较前馈Transformer（BERT）和循环Transformer（ALBERT）在持续学习任务上的表现，分析其学习到的计算解决方案。

Result: BERT学习捷径解，限制泛化且无法正向迁移到新经验；ALBERT学习类似For循环的解，CL性能更好。但两种模型在需要跨经验组合的CL任务中均失败。ALBERT可通过跨经验数据混合训练挽救性能下降，BERT则无法挽救。

Conclusion: 循环Transformer（ALBERT）可能具有更适合持续学习的归纳偏置，未来应进一步研究Transformer架构与涌现计算解决方案之间的相互作用。

Abstract: Identifying and exploiting common features across domains is at the heart of the human ability to make analogies, and is believed to be crucial for the ability to continually learn. To do this successfully, general and flexible computational strategies must be developed. While the extent to which Transformer neural network models can perform compositional reasoning has been the subject of intensive recent investigation, little work has been done to systematically understand how well these models can leverage their representations to learn new, related experiences. To address this gap, we expand the previously developed Learning Equality and Group Operations (LEGO) framework to a continual learning (CL) setting ("continual LEGO"). Using this continual LEGO experimental paradigm, we study the capability of feedforward and recurrent Transformer models to perform CL. We find that BERT, a canonical feedforward Transformer model, learns shortcut solutions that limits its ability to generalize and prevents strong forward transfer to new experiences. In contrast, we find evidence supporting the hypothesis that ALBERT, a recurrent version of BERT, learns a For loop-esque solution, which leads to better CL performance. When applying BERT and ALBERT models to a CL setting that requires composition across experiences, we find that both model families fail. Our investigation suggests that ALBERT models can have their performance drop rescued by use of training strategies that combine data across experiences, but this is not true for BERT models, where a detrimental shortcut solution becomes entrenched with initial training. Our results demonstrate that the recurrent ALBERT model may have an inductive bias better suited for CL and motivate future investigation of the interplay between Transformer architecture and computational solutions that emerge in modern models and tasks.

</details>


### [180] [Online Localized Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05497)
*Yuheng Lai,Garvesh Raskutti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出在线局部共形预测（OLCP）及其变体OLCP-Hedge，通过结合在线自适应与协变量依赖的局部化策略，在非交换数据（如时间序列）场景下实现更窄的预测集和长期有效的覆盖率保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线共形方法（如ACI）依赖全局校准，在协变量异质性环境下效率低下，无法反映局部变化。

Method: OLCP：将在线自适应与协变量依赖的局部化结合；OLCP-Hedge：将带宽选择建模为在线专家聚合问题，采用约束在线凸优化框架。

Result: 两种算法均提供覆盖保证；仿真和真实数据实验表明，相比现有基线方法，能在维持有效长期覆盖率的同时得到更窄的预测集。

Conclusion: OLCP及其变体有效解决了非交换数据下共形预测的效率问题，兼顾了长期有效性与局部异质性适应能力。

Abstract: Conformal prediction is a framework that provides valid uncertainty quantification for general models with exchangeable data. However, in the online learning and time-series settings, exchangeability is not satisfied. Existing online conformal methods, such as adaptive conformal inference (ACI), can achieve long-run validity, yet they remain inefficient under covariate heterogeneity because they rely on global calibration. We propose \emph{Online Localized Conformal Prediction (OLCP)}, which combines online adaptation with covariate-dependent localization to better reflect heterogeneity. To reduce sensitivity to the localization bandwidth, we further develop \emph{OLCP-Hedge}, which performs bandwidth selection as an online expert aggregation problem using a constrained online convex optimization framework. Importantly, we provide coverage guarantees for both algorithms and demonstrate through simulations and real-data experiments that the proposed methods attain valid long-run coverage with narrower prediction sets than existing baselines.

</details>


### [181] [Non-Myopic Active Feature Acquisition via Pathwise Policy Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05511)
*Linus Aronsson,Morteza Haghir Chehreghani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出非短视路径策略梯度（NM-PPG）方法，通过连续松弛和直通式推演机制，在部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）框架下实现主动特征获取（AFA）的端到端非短视优化，实验表明优于现有基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动特征获取（AFA）问题中特征获取有成本，需自适应决策何时获取哪些特征以及何时停止预测。现有方法存在方差高、短视优化等问题，亟需一种低方差、非短视的端到端优化方法。

Method: 1) 将AFA建模为POMDP；2) 引入连续松弛使获取轨迹可微，实现路径梯度优化；3) 提出直通式推演方案：前向采用硬特征获取，反向传播通过对应的软松弛；4) 采用熵正则化与分阶段温度锐化稳定训练。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实数据集上，NM-PPG均优于现有最先进的AFA基线方法。

Conclusion: NM-PPG通过连续松弛和直通式推演有效降低了策略梯度方差，实现了非短视的端到端优化，为AFA问题提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Active feature acquisition (AFA) considers prediction problems in which features are costly to obtain and the learner adaptively decides which feature values to acquire for each instance and when to stop and predict. AFA can be formulated as a partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP), which naturally admits a sequential decision-making perspective. In this paper, we present non-myopic pathwise policy gradients (NM-PPG), a new AFA method built around this formulation. We introduce a continuous relaxation of the acquisition process that enables pathwise gradients through the full acquisition trajectory, avoiding the high variance of standard score-function policy gradients while allowing end-to-end optimization of a non-myopic acquisition policy. To better align training with deployment, we further develop a straight-through rollout scheme that follows hard feature acquisitions in the forward pass while backpropagating through the corresponding soft relaxation in the backward pass. We stabilize optimization with entropy regularization and staged temperature sharpening. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that NM-PPG yields superior performance relative to state-of-the-art AFA baselines.

</details>


### [182] [OpenG2G: A Simulation Platform for AI Datacenter-Grid Runtime Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05519)
*Jae-Won Chung,Zhirui Liang,Yanyong Mao,Jiasi Chen,Mosharaf Chowdhury,Vladimir Dvorkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenG2G，一个面向AI数据中心与电网运行时协调的仿真平台，通过模块化架构支持多种控制范式（经典控制、优化控制、学习控制），帮助量化AI模型与部署选择对数据中心灵活性的影响，缓解大规模数据中心给电网带来的容量与可靠性压力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI算力需求激增和新数据中心建设给电网带来巨大容量和可靠性挑战，导致数据中心并网延迟达数年之久，制约AI发展。现有研究缺乏一个能够系统评估数据中心-电网协调策略的仿真平台。
