### [291] [FedFrozen: Two-Stage Federated Optimization via Attention Kernel Freezing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06446)
*Junye Du,Zhenghao Li,Yushi Feng,Long Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedFrozen两阶段联邦优化框架：先全模型热身训练，再冻结注意力机制的query/key模块仅优化value模块，以缓解异构联邦学习中的客户端漂移问题。通过线性注意力公式解析了该方法的优化机理，并在仿真和真实数据上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构联邦学习中客户端漂移问题严重，现有方法多通过目标正则化或更新校正来解决。近期观察到Transformer架构在异构联邦训练中天然更鲁棒，因此探究注意力机制中不同参数组件对联邦优化的影响。

Method: 将注意力模块分解为query/key块（决定注意力核）和value块（在核作用下进行语义变换）。提出FedFrozen两阶段框架：第一阶段全模型热身训练；第二阶段冻结query/key块，仅优化value块。在线性注意力公式下分析了两个阶段的优化行为。

Result: 仿真实验验证了预测的偏差-漂移行为；真实数据实验表明FedFrozen在异构联邦学习中提升了Transformer模型的稳定性和有效性。

Conclusion: 注意力机制中query/key和value组件在联邦优化中扮演不同角色，冻结query/key可有效缓解客户端漂移；热身阶段长度存在显式的权衡取舍。

Abstract: Federated learning with heterogeneous clients remains a significant challenge for deep learning, primarily due to client drift arising from inconsistent local updates. Existing federated optimization methods typically address this issue through objective-level regularization or update-correction mechanisms. Recent studies, however, suggest that Transformer-based architectures may be inherently more robust than conventional models under heterogeneous federated training. Motivated by this observation, we investigate how different parameter components within the attention mechanism influence federated optimization. Specifically, we decompose the attention module into a query/key block, which determines the attention kernel, and a value block, which performs semantic transformation under the induced kernel. Based on this perspective, we propose FedFrozen, a two-stage federated optimization framework that first performs full-model warm-up training and then freezes the query/key block while continuing to optimize the value block. Under a linear-attention formulation, we show that the warm-up stage can be interpreted as an inexact descent procedure on a regularized kernel-profile objective, while the frozen stage reduces to a restricted value-block optimization problem under a fixed attention kernel. Our analysis further reveals an explicit trade-off that governs the choice of warm-up length. Simulations validate the predicted bias-drift behavior, and real-data experiments demonstrate that FedFrozen improves both the stability and effectiveness of Transformer models in heterogeneous federated learning.

</details>


### [292] [Scene-Adaptive Continual Learning for CSI-based Human Activity Recognition with Mixture of Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06447)
*Wenhan Zheng,Yuyi Mao,Ivan Wang-Hei Ho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SAMoE-C方法，通过注意力路由的混合专家系统实现跨场景CSI人体活动识别的域自适应，仅需少量回放缓冲区即可达到SOTA精度且显著降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于CSI的人体活动识别在不同物理环境下面临域偏移导致的性能下降问题，现有持续学习方法存在扩展性差、依赖大容量回放缓冲区或推理成本线性增长等缺陷。

Method: 提出场景自适应混合专家模型（SAMoE-C），包含：(1) 基于注意力机制的语义路由器，为每个输入仅激活选中的专家子集；(2) 聚类专家模块，实现场景特定适配；(3) 新型训练协议，仅需极小的回放缓冲区稳定路由器的域判别能力。

Result: 在四场景CSI数据集上，SAMoE-C达到与SOTA相当的精度，同时推理成本显著低于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过模块化专家、选择性激活路由器和轻量训练协议的联合设计，SAMoE-C实现了可扩展的跨域CSI-HAR部署，兼具低训练开销和高计算效率，适用于真实场景。

Abstract: Channel state information (CSI)-based human activity recognition (HAR) is vulnerable to performance degradation under domain shifts across varying physical environments. Continual learning (CL) offers a principled way to learn new domains sequentially while preserving past knowledge, but existing CL solutions for CSI-based HAR scale poorly with accumulating domains, rely on a large replay buffer, or incur linearly growing inference cost. In this letter, we propose Scene-Adaptive Mixture of Experts with Clustered Specialists (SAMoE-C), which formulates cross-domain CSI-based HAR as a mixture-of-experts system that enables scene-specific adaptation, via an attention-based semantic router that activates only selected experts for each input. Moreover, we develop a novel training protocol, which requires only a tiny replay buffer for stabilizing domain discrimination of the router. Experimental results on a four-scene CSI dataset demonstrate that SAMoE-C approaches the state-of-the-art accuracy, while maintaining a significantly lower inference cost. By jointly combining modular experts, selective activation with router and a lightweight training protocol, SAMoE-C enables scalable cross-domain CSI-based HAR deployment with low training overhead and high computational efficiency in real-world settings.

</details>


### [293] [Diversity Curves for Graph Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06466)
*Katharina Limbeck,Nadja Häusermann,Martin Carrasco,Guy Wolf,Bastian Rieck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多样性曲线（diversity curves）作为图级表示方法，通过追踪图在不同粗化层级上的结构多样性来生成可解释、可扩展且可直接比较的图嵌入，并利用边收缩粗化提升表达能力，在多个实际任务中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图级表示方法在处理不同基数的图（即使来自同一分布）时存在困难，无监督任务尤其需要可解释、可扩展且可靠的、考虑图大小的表示方法。

Method: 通过追踪图在粗化层级上的结构多样性，定义多样性曲线作为图嵌入；利用边收缩粗化（edge contraction coarsening）并证明其能提升表达能力；引入"spread of graphs"作为新的等距不变量，用于编码图的度量多样性和几何结构。

Result: 在四类任务中优于基线方法：(i) 聚类和可视化不同规模的模拟图，(ii) 区分单细胞图的几何结构，(iii) 比较分子图数据集的结构，(iv) 表征几何形状。

Conclusion: 多样性曲线作为图级表示具有可解释性、高效性和跨粗化层级直接可比性，边收缩粗化增强了表达能力，使得该方法优于单纯的结构描述符。

Abstract: Graph-level representations are crucial tools for characterising structural differences between graphs. However, comparing graphs with different cardinalities, even when sampled from the same underlying distribution, remains challenging. Unsupervised tasks in particular require interpretable, scalable, and reliable size-aware graph representations. Our work addresses these issues by tracking the structural diversity of a graph across coarsening levels. The resulting graph embeddings, which we denote diversity curves, are interpretable by construction, efficient, and directly comparable across coarsening hierarchies. Specifically, we track the spread of graphs, a novel isometry invariant that is inherently well-suited for encoding the metric diversity and geometry of graphs. We utilise edge contraction coarsening and prove that this improves expressivity, thus leading to more powerful graph-level representations than structural descriptors alone. Demonstrating their utility over a range of baseline methods in practice, we use diversity curves to (i) cluster and visualise simulated graphs across varying sizes, (ii) distinguish the geometry of single-cell graphs, (iii) compare the structure of molecular graph datasets, and (iv) characterise geometric shapes.

</details>


### [294] [No Triangulation Without Representation: Generalization in Topological Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06467)
*Johannes S. Schmidt,Martin Carrasco,Ernst Röell,Guy Wolf,Nello Blaser,Bastian Rieck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文扩展了MANTRA基准数据集至更多样化的流形类别，发现GNN和HOMP方法在适当表示和特征分配下均可饱和该基准，提出基于表示多样性和三角剖分细化的新评估协议，并指出现有模型无法泛化至数据组合结构之外，揭示了拓扑感知归纳偏置的研究空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑深度学习模型日益增多，但缺乏统一的评估标准；拓扑对象允许图数据中不适用的操作（如结构精化），导致评估困难。

Method: 将MANTRA基准扩展至更丰富的流形同胚类型；系统研究表示和特征分配对模型性能的影响；提出基于表示多样性和三角剖分细化的新评估协议。

Result: GNN和HOMP方法在正确表示和特征分配下均可饱和基准；现有模型无法泛化到数据组合结构之外。

Conclusion: 现有拓扑深度学习模型缺乏拓扑结构理解能力，亟需开发具有拓扑感知归纳偏置的模型；本文为未来评估提供了框架。

Abstract: Despite an ever-increasing interest in topological deep learning models that target higher-order datasets, there is no consensus on how to evaluate such models. This is exacerbated by the fact that topological objects permit operations, such as structural refinements, that are not appropriate for graph data. In this work, we extend MANTRA, a benchmark dataset containing manifold triangulations, to a larger class of manifolds with more diverse homeomorphism types. We show that, unlike prior claims, both graph neural networks (GNNs) and higher-order message passing (HOMP) methods can saturate the benchmark. However, we find that this is contingent on the right representation and feature assignment, emphasizing their importance in baseline models. We thus provide a novel evaluation protocol based on representational diversity and triangulation refinement. Surprisingly, we find no indication that existing models are capable of generalizing beyond the combinatorial structure of the data. This points towards a research gap in developing models that understand topological structure independent of scale. Our work thus provides the necessary scaffolding to evaluate future models and enable the development of topology-aware inductive biases.

</details>


### [295] [Hitting Time Isomorphism for Multi-Stage Planning with Foundation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06470)
*Magnus Victor Boock,Abdullah Akgül,Mustafa Mert Çelikok,Melih Kandemir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于算子理论的离线强化学习表示学习框架，通过学习受控马尔可夫过程的击中时间观测来恢复有向时间几何结构。该框架学习希尔伯特空间位移几何，其中期望击中时间被实现为潜在位移的线性泛函。基于此理论，作者提出了同构嵌入学习（IEL）算法，通过显式击中时间回归确保学习到的几何反映实际决策时间进展，在离线迷宫 locomotion 数据上实现了最先进的 foundation policy 学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常产生对称距离或不满足三角不等式，无法捕捉受控马尔可夫过程的真实有向时间几何结构，限制了离线强化学习中长期规划的能力。

Method: 提出算子理论表示学习框架，在潜在线性闭包条件下证明表示存在且唯一可识（up to 有界线性同构）；推导全局击中时间误差与单步转移误差及环境瞬态谱半径的关系；基于此理论设计IEL算法，结合HILP风格一致性目标和显式击中时间回归。

Result: 在离线迷宫 locomotion 数据上，IEL 改进了 foundation policy 学习的最新方法，展示了非对称组合结构在基于图的多阶段长时域规划中的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文提供了理论保证（有限样本保证涵盖近似误差、统计复杂度和轨迹标签不匹配），并验证了所提方法在实际离线强化学习任务中的有效性。

Abstract: We present a new operator-theoretic representation learning framework for offline reinforcement learning that recovers the directed temporal geometry of a controlled Markov process from hitting time observations. While prior art often produces symmetric distances or fails to satisfy the triangle inequality, our framework learns a Hilbert-space displacement geometry where expected hitting times are realized as linear functionals of latent displacements. We prove that this representation exists under latent linear closure and is uniquely identifiable up to a bounded linear isomorphism. For finite-dimensional implementations, we show that global hitting-time error is bounded by one-step transition error amplified by the environment's transient spectral radius. Furthermore, we provide finite-sample guarantees accounting for approximation, statistical complexity, and trajectory-label mismatch. Derived from this theory, we curate Isomorphic Embedding Learning (IEL) as a new goal-agnostic foundation policy learning algorithm that anchors a HILP-style consistency objective with explicit hitting-time regression to ensure that the learned geometry reflects actual decision-time progress. This asymmetric and compositional structure enables robust graph-based multi-stage planning for long-horizon navigation. Our experiments demonstrate that IEL improves the state of the art of learning foundation policy policies from offline maze locomotion data. Our code can be found on https://github.com/MagnusBoock/IEL

</details>


### [296] [Efficient Serving for Dynamic Agent Workflows with Prediction-based KV-Cache Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06472)
*Haoyu Zheng,Fangcheng Fu,Jia Wu,Binhang Yuan,Yongqiang Zhang,Hao Wang,Yuanyuan Zhu,Xiao Yan,Jiawei Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PBKV是一个基于预测的KV-Cache管理系统，通过预测动态工作流中的智能体调用序列来优化缓存复用，在动态工作流上相比LRU实现最高1.85倍加速，在静态工作流上相比SOTA基线KVFlow实现最高1.26倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV-Cache管理方法要么在智能体级别管理，无法利用工作流内的复用机会；要么在工作流级别管理但假设工作流调用静态的智能体序列。然而实际工作流是动态的，智能体调用序列及缓存复用机会取决于每个任务的上下文，亟需支持动态工作流的高效KV-Cache管理系统。

Method: PBKV通过融合历史工作流引导和目标工作流上下文，预测未来若干步的智能体调用；基于预测估计缓存条目的复用潜力，将高潜力条目保留在GPU内存中；在缓存驱逐和预取时保守地利用预测结果以应对预测误差。

Result: 在三个工作流基准上，PBKV在动态工作流上相比LRU实现最高1.85倍加速，在静态工作流上相比SOTA基线KVFlow实现最高1.26倍加速。

Conclusion: PBKV通过预测驱动的KV-Cache管理策略，有效解决了动态工作流中缓存复用效率低下的问题，兼顾了动态和静态工作流的性能优化。

Abstract: LLM-based workflows compose specialized agents to execute complex tasks, and these agents usually share substantial context, allowing KV-Cache reuse to save computation. Existing approaches either manage KV-Cache at agent level and fail to exploit the reuse opportunities within workflows, or manage cache at the workflow level but assume that each workflow calls a static sequence of agents. However, practical workflows are typically dynamic, where the sequence of invoked agents and thus induced cache reuse opportunities depend on the context of each task. To serve such dynamic workflows efficiently, we build a system dubbed PBKV (\textbf{P}rediction-\textbf{B}ased \textbf{KV}-Cache Management). For each workflow, PBKV predicts the agent invocations in several future steps by fusing the guidance from historical workflows and context of the target workflow. Based on the predictions, PBKV estimates the reuse potential of cache entries and keeps the high-potential entries in GPU memory. To be robust to prediction errors, PBKV utilizes the predictions conservatively during both cache eviction and prefetching. Experiments on three workflow benchmarks show that PBKV achieves up to $1.85\times$ speedup over LRU on dynamic workflows, and up to $1.26\times$ speedup over the SOTA baseline KVFlow on the static workflow.

</details>


### [297] [Q-MMR: Off-Policy Evaluation via Recursive Reweighting and Moment Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06474)
*Xiang Li,Nan Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Q-MMR框架，通过数据点重加权实现离策略评估，仅需Q^π可实现性即可获得维数无关的有限样本保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离策略评估方法要么依赖于重要性采样的方差问题，要么需要较强的函数类复杂度假设，缺乏一种通用且理论保证简洁的方法。

Method: 通过矩匹配目标学习每个数据点的标量权重，使得重加权后的奖励近似目标策略下的期望回报。权重以自顶向下归纳方式学习，并使用价值函数判别器类。

Result: 建立了仅依赖Q^π可实现性的数据依赖有限样本保证，误差界与函数类的统计复杂度无关（维数无关），并与重要性采样、线性FQE等方法建立了联系。

Conclusion: Q-MMR为离策略评估提供了新颖的理论视角，同时揭示了对offline RL中覆盖率概念的新理解。

Abstract: We present a novel theoretical framework, Q-MMR, for off-policy evaluation in finite-horizon MDPs. Q-MMR learns a set of scalar weights, one for each data point, such that the reweighted rewards approximate the expected return under the target policy. The weights are learned inductively in a top-down manner via a moment matching objective against a value-function discriminator class. Notably, and perhaps surprisingly, a data-dependent finite-sample guarantee for general function approximation can be established under only the realizability of $Q^π$, with a dimension-free bound -- that is, the error does not depend on the statistical complexity of the function class. We also establish connections to several existing methods, such as importance sampling and linear FQE. Further theoretical analyses shed new light on the nature of coverage, a concept of fundamental importance to offline RL.

</details>


### [298] [Operator-Guided Invariance Learning for Continuous Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06500)
*Zuyuan Zhang,Fei Xu Yu,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VPSD-RL框架，通过Lie群作用与pullback算子发现连续时间强化学习中的值保持结构，包括精确与近似结构，并从理论上证明了HJB残差小可保证近似值保持结构的稳定性，实验表明该方法提升了数据效率与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间/状态/动作空间的强化学习通常数据需求大且对 nuisance 变化和漂移敏感，现有方法多局限于预设对称性与严格等变性，缺乏发现更一般值保持结构的能力。

Method: 将连续RL建模为受控扩散过程，通过Lie群作用与pullback算子定义值保持映射；推导值保持结构存在的充要条件（值函数pullback与动作pushforward与受控生成元及奖励泛函交换）；通过HJB残差小给出近似值保持结构的严格保证；拟合可微的漂移、扩散和奖励模型；通过判定方程残差最小化学习无穷小生成元；通过ODE流指数映射获得有限变换；通过过渡增强和变换一致性正则化集成到连续RL中。

Result: 证明了有界生成元/奖励残差意味着最优值函数沿近似轨道的定量稳定性，灵敏度由有效horizon决定；在连续控制基准上观察到数据效率与鲁棒性的提升。

Conclusion: VPSD-RL为发现连续RL中的值保持结构提供了理论统一框架，能够自动学习精确与近似对称结构，提升学习的效率与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) with continuous time and state/action spaces is often data-intensive and brittle under nuisance variability and shift, motivating methods that exploit value-preserving structures to stabilize and improve learning. Most existing approaches focus on special cases, such as prescribed symmetries and exact equivariance, without addressing how to discover more general structures that require nonlinear operators to transform and map between continuous state/action systems with isomorphic value functions. We propose \textbf{VPSD-RL} (Value-Preserving Structure Discovery for Reinforcement Learning). It models continuous RL as a controlled diffusion with value-preserving mappings defined through Lie-group actions and associated pullback operators. We show that a value-preserving structure exists exactly when pulling back the value function and pushing forward actions commute with the controlled generator and reward functional. Further, approximate value-preserving structures with rigorous guarantees can be found when the Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman mismatch is small. This framework discovers exact and approximate value-preserving structures by searching for the associated Lie group operators. VPSD-RL fits differentiable drift, diffusion, and reward models; learns infinitesimal generators via determining-equation residual minimization; exponentiates them with ODE flows to obtain finite transformations; and integrates them into continuous RL through transition augmentation and transformation-consistency regularization. We show that bounded generator/reward mismatch implies quantitative stability of the optimal value function along approximate orbits, with sensitivity governed by the effective horizon, and observe improved data efficiency and robustness on continuous-control benchmarks.

</details>


### [299] [PACZero: PAC-Private Fine-Tuning of Language Models via Sign Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06505)
*Murat Bilgehan Ertan,Xiaochen Zhu,Phuong Ha Nguyen,Marten van Dijk,Srinivas Devadas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PACZero提出了一族PAC-私有的零阶机制用于微调大语言模型，通过在$I(S^*; Y_{1:T})=0$（即零互信息）的高隐私预算下实现可用效用。核心思想是利用PAC Privacy仅在秘密子集影响发布时才计入互信息的特点，通过符号量化子集聚合的零阶梯度来创建一致性步骤（所有候选子集更新方向一致），从而在这些步骤实现零条件互信息。提出两个变体：PACZero-MI（预算互信息）和PACZero-ZPL（零互信息，通过在分歧步骤投均匀硬币）。在SST-2和SQuAD上使用OPT-1.3B和OPT-6.7B进行LoRA和全参数微调实验，在$I=0$时SST-2准确率达88.99%，仅比非私有MeZO基线低2.1个百分点，远超现有DP方法在高隐私区间的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私（DP）框架在高隐私区间（$\varepsilon<1$）需要注入极大噪声导致可用效用丧失，而成员推理攻击（MIA）的防御需求日益迫切。作者希望设计一种能在零互信息（即MIA后验成功率等于先验）的高隐私保护水平下仍保持可用效用的微调方法。

Method: 1. 利用PAC Privacy的核心特性：互信息仅在发布结果依赖于"哪个候选子集是秘密"时才被计入；2. 对子集聚合的零阶梯度进行符号量化，检测所有候选子集是否对更新方向达成一致（unanimity）；3. 在一致性步骤，发布的符号与秘密无关，产生零条件互信息；4. 两个变体：PACZero-MI通过精确校准二进制发布分配互信息预算，PACZero-ZPL在分歧步骤使用均匀随机硬币翻转实现严格零互信息。

Result: 在SST-2上OPT-1.3B全参数微调$I=0$时达到88.99%准确率，仅比非私有的MeZO基线（91.1%）低2.1个百分点。在SST-2和SQuAD任务上，OPT-1.3B和OPT-6.7B在LoRA和全参数设置下均取得有竞争力的结果。在$\varepsilon<1$的高隐私区间，没有现有方法能产生可用效用，而PACZero-ZPL在$I=0$时仍保持良好性能。

Conclusion: PACZero首次在零互信息（相当于DP的$\varepsilon=0$且无穷大噪声）的高隐私水平下实现了可用效用，通过利用一致性子集聚合梯度的符号量化策略，有效打破了隐私保护与实用性能之间的根本权衡。该方法在MIA防御方面比DP框架更为优越，为LLM隐私微调开辟了新路径。

Abstract: We introduce PACZero, a family of PAC-private zeroth-order mechanisms for fine-tuning large language models that delivers usable utility at $I(S^*; Y_{1:T})=0$. This privacy regime bounds the membership-inference attack (MIA) posterior success rate at the prior, an MIA-resistance level the DP framework matches only at $\varepsilon=0$ and infinite noise. All DP-ZO comparisons below are matched at the MIA posterior level. The key insight is that PAC Privacy charges mutual information only when the release depends on which candidate subset is the secret. Sign-quantizing subset-aggregated zeroth-order gradients creates frequent unanimity, steps at which every candidate subset agrees on the update direction; at these steps the released sign costs zero conditional mutual information. We propose two variants that span the privacy-utility trade-off: PACZero-MI (budgeted MI via exact calibration on the binary release) and PACZero-ZPL ($I=0$ via a uniform coin flip on disagreement steps). We evaluate on SST-2 and SQuAD with OPT-1.3B and OPT-6.7B in both LoRA and full-parameter tracks. On SST-2 OPT-1.3B full fine-tuning at $I=0$, PACZero-ZPL reaches ${88.99\pm0.91}$, within $2.1$pp of the non-private MeZO baseline ($91.1$ FT). No prior method produces usable utility in the high-privacy regime $\varepsilon<1$, and PACZero-ZPL obtains competitive SST-2 accuracy and nontrivial SQuAD F1 across OPT-1.3B and OPT-6.7B at $I=0$.

</details>


### [300] [Is One Layer Enough? Understanding Inference Dynamics in Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06510)
*Amir Rezaei Balef,Mykhailo Koshil,Katharina Eggensperger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次大规模研究了6个基于Transformer的表格基础模型在上下文学习中的逐层动态机制，发现深度冗余和重叠计算，并据此设计了一个仅需原模型20%参数的单层循环模型，性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer-based tabular foundation models (TFMs)在小到中型表格预测基准任务中表现优异，但其推理机制尚未被深入探索。

Method: 对6个最新的表格上下文学习模型进行大规模逐层动态机制研究，分析预测随深度涌现的过程，识别不同的推理阶段，揭示潜在空间动态；基于发现的深度冗余设计单层循环模型。

Result: 发现多个模型存在显著的深度冗余，推理阶段存在迭代精炼和重叠计算；设计的单层循环模型仅用20%参数即达到可比性能。

Conclusion: 表格基础模型的深度层存在冗余，可通过循环单层架构大幅压缩参数而不损失性能。

Abstract: Transformer-based tabular foundation models (TFMs) dominate small to medium tabular predictive benchmark tasks, yet their inference mechanisms remain largely unexplored. We present the first large-scale mechanistic study of layerwise dynamics in 6 state-of-the-art tabular in-context learning models. We explore how predictions emerge across depth, identify distinct stages of inference and reveal latent-space dynamics that differ from those of language models. Our findings indicate substantial depthwise redundancy across multiple models, suggesting iterative refinement with overlapping computations during inference stages. Guided by these insights, we design a proof-of-concept, looped single-layer model that uses only 20% of the original model's parameters while achieving comparable performance. The code is available at https://github.com/amirbalef/is_one_layer_enough.

</details>


### [301] [On the Implicit Reward Overfitting and the Low-rank Dynamics in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06523)
*Hao Ye,Jisheng Dang,Junfeng Fang,Bimei Wang,Yizhou Zhang,Ning Lv,Wencan Zhang,Hong Peng,Bin Hu,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现强化学习（RLVR）的推理能力主要集中在秩-1分量上，并提出周期性秩-1替换方法，揭示了RLVR存在隐式奖励过拟合、知识非保持性、奇异值重尾分布以及左奇异向量对齐倾向等特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明RLVR增强的推理能力集中在秩-1分量上，但对其背后的机理理解不足，需要深入分析RLVR如何塑造模型参数及其潜在问题。

Method: 采用周期性秩-1替换（Periodic Rank-1 Substitution）方法，通过分析和对比训练过程中奖励与测试性能的关系，以及观察奇异值分布、奇异向量对齐等统计特性来揭示RLVR的内在机制。

Result: 发现了三个关键性质：(1) RLVR的秩-1分量仅保留数学推理能力而不保留其他模型知识；(2) RLVR通过优化特定奇异谱实现，全连接层的奇异值呈重尾分布；(3) 秩-1分量的左奇异向量在训练中表现出更强的对齐趋势，表明RLVR本质上是优化采样效率。

Conclusion: 上述发现揭示了RLVR如何塑造模型参数，为改进现有RL范式或其他训练范式以实现持续学习提供了潜在见解。

Abstract: Recent extensive research has demonstrated that the enhanced reasoning capabilities acquired by models through Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) are primarily concentrated within the rank-1 components. Predicated on this observation, we employed Periodic Rank-1 Substitution and identified a counterintuitive phenomenon: RLVR may exhibit implicit reward overfitting to the training dataset. Specifically, the model can achieve satisfactory performance on the test set even when its rewards remain relatively low during the training process. Furthermore, we characterize three distinct properties of RL training: (1) The effective rank-1 component in RLVR don't maintain other model knowledge except mathematical reasoning capability. (2) RLVR fundamentally functions by optimizing a specific singular spectrum. The distribution of singular values of almost all linear layers in RLVR-trained model behaves like heavy-tailed distribution. (3) the left singular vectors associated with rank-1 components demonstrate a stronger alignment tendency during training, which echoes the discovery that RLVR is optimizing sampling efficiency in essence. Taken together, our findings and analysis further reveal how RLVR shapes model parameters and offer potential insights for improving existing RL paradigms or other training paradigms to implement continual learning.

</details>


### [302] [Diffusion-Based Posterior Sampling: A Feynman-Kac Analysis of Bias and Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06538)
*Matias G. Delgadino,Sebastien Motsch,Advait Parulekar,William Porteous,Sanjay Shakkottai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过引入可处理的替代路径和Feynman-Kac表示，系统刻画了基于扩散的后验采样器（如DPS和STSL）的偏差来源，揭示了偏差与条件协方差和奖励曲率的依赖关系，并解释了早期引导停止等修正策略的原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管扩散后验采样器在逆问题中被广泛使用，但其理论行为（如偏差和低温不稳定性）尚不清晰，缺乏对其偏差的严格刻画和理解。

Method: 引入一个连接真实后验与标准高斯的可处理替代路径，与采样器路径进行对比；利用密度比满足的抛物型偏微分方程中的反应项衡量累积偏差，并通过Feynman-Kac表示给出Radon-Nikodym修正的显式路径期望表达。

Result: 对于DPS，修正项是Ornstein-Uhlenbeck路径期望，耦合了数据条件协方差与奖励曲率，揭示了过采样和欠采样的区域；STSL通过辅助漂移将轨迹引向低不确定性区域，削弱了DPS反应项的空间变化部分；早期引导停止可缓解前向欧拉积分导致的低温不稳定性。

Conclusion: 这些结果阐明了采样器偏差的来源，解释了现有的修正策略，并为设计稳定的变体提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Diffusion-based posterior samplers use pretrained diffusion priors to sample from measurement- or reward-conditioned posteriors, and are widely used for inverse problems. Yet their theoretical behavior remains poorly understood: even with exact prior scores, their outputs are biased, and in low-temperature regimes their discretizations can become unstable. We characterize this bias by introducing a tractable surrogate path connecting the true posterior to a standard Gaussian and comparing it to the sampler's path. Their density ratio satisfies a parabolic PDE whose reaction term measures the accumulated bias. A Feynman-Kac representation then expresses the Radon-Nikodym correction as an explicit path expectation, identifying which posterior regions are over- or under-sampled.
  We apply this framework to DPS and STSL, a related sampler. For DPS, the correction is an Ornstein-Uhlenbeck path expectation coupling the data conditional covariance with the reward curvature, revealing where DPS over- or under-samples. Next, we reinterpret STSL as an auxiliary drift that steers trajectories toward low-uncertainty regions, flattening the spatially varying part of the DPS reaction term. Finally, we characterize early guidance-stopping, a common mitigation for low-temperature instabilities caused by forward-Euler integration of the vector field. Together, these results clarify sampler bias, explain existing correctives, and guide stable variant designs.

</details>


### [303] [Hedging Memory Horizons for Non-Stationary Prediction via Online Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06541)
*Yutong Wang,Yannig Goude,Qiwei Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MELO方法，通过记忆对冲的指数加权最小二乘在线聚合，在无需先验知识的情况下自适应处理分布漂移，并在法国电力负荷预测中 outperforms 基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线预测中面临分布漂移挑战，预测器需在平稳期保持稳定、在变化期快速适应，且最优适应记忆长度未知，现有方法难以同时兼顾。

Method: MELO方法：对非预期的基预测器池，使用多个遗忘因子的EWLS专家进行不同尺度的适应，再通过无参数在线聚合规则MLpol聚合原始预测和EWLS适应后的预测。

Result: 在法国电力负荷预测（含COVID-19封锁期）中，无需任何外部政策指标，MELO相对于纯MLpol降低RMSE 34.7%，且优于使用外部COVID政策协变量的TabICL参考方法。

Conclusion: MELO是一种轻量级、无模型假设的在线预测方法，能够自适应不同尺度的分布漂移，具有理论保证和实际效果。

Abstract: We study online prediction under distribution shift, where inputs arrive chronologically and outcomes are revealed only after prediction. In this setting, predictors must remain stable in quiet regimes yet adapt when regimes shift, and the right adaptation memory is unknown in advance. We propose MELO (Memory-hedged Exponentially Weighted Least-Squares Online aggregation), a model-agnostic method that hedges across adaptation scales: it wraps any non-anticipating base-predictor pool with exponentially weighted least-squares (EWLS) adaptation experts at multiple forgetting factors, and aggregates raw and EWLS-adapted forecasts with MLpol, a parameter-free online aggregation rule. Under boundedness conditions, we establish deterministic oracle inequalities showing that it competes with both the best raw predictor and the best bounded, time-varying affine combinations of the base predictions, up to a path-length-dependent tracking cost and a sublinear aggregation overhead. We evaluate MELO on French national electricity-load forecasting through the COVID-19 lockdown using no regime indicators, lockdown dates, or policy covariates. MELO reduces overall RMSE by 34.7\% relative to base-only MLpol and achieves lower overall RMSE than a TabICL reference supplied with an external COVID policy-response covariate. Moreover, MELO requires only lightweight per-step recursive updates without model retraining.

</details>


### [304] [Sequential Design of Genetic Circuits Under Uncertainty With Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06552)
*Michal Kobiela,Diego A. Oyarzún,Michael U. Gutmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于强化学习的序贯框架，用于在分子随机性和跨实验室条件不确定性下优化基因电路。通过预训练的摊销策略，该方法避免了传统贝叶斯方法中昂贵的推理和优化步骤，实现了基于观测的即时适应。在异源基因表达和repressilator电路模型上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物系统设计受限于两种不确定性：生物分子反应的内在随机性，以及不同实验室或实验条件之间的变异性。现有贝叶斯方法需在每轮实验后进行耗时的推理和优化，导致延迟。

Method: 采用基于微分方程或马尔可夫跳变过程的仿真器模型，结合强化学习策略方法；通过预训练在可能的未知参数分布上进行摊销，避免显式参数推断。

Result: 在异源基因表达和repressilator电路模型上验证了方法在处理分子噪声和跨实验室变异性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的序贯框架能够高效处理两种不确定性，且预训练摊销策略克服了传统贝叶斯方法的计算瓶颈，实现了即时适应。

Abstract: The design of biological systems is hindered by uncertainty arising from both intrinsic stochasticity of biomolecular reactions and variability across laboratory or experimental conditions. In this work, we present a sequential framework to optimize genetic circuits under both forms of uncertainty. By employing simulator models based on differential equations or Markov jump processes alongside a reinforcement learning (RL) policy-based approach, our method suggests experiments that adapt to unknown laboratory conditions while accounting for inherent stochasticity. While previous Bayesian methods address uncertainty through iterative experiment-inference-optimization cycles, they typically require computationally expensive inference and optimization steps after each experimental round, leading to delays. To overcome this bottleneck, we propose an amortized approach trained up-front across a distribution of possible uncertain parameters. This strategy sidesteps the need for explicit parameter inference during the design cycle, enabling immediate, observation-based adaptation. We demonstrate our framework on models for heterologous gene expression and a repressilator circuit, showing that it efficiently handles both molecular noise and cross-laboratory variability.

</details>


### [305] [Diverse Sampling in Diffusion Models with Marginal Preserving Particle Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06553)
*Gal Vinograd,Idan Achituve,Ethan Fetaya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EDDY是一种无需额外训练的引导机制，通过散度自由动力学在保持边缘分布的同时提升扩散模型与流匹配模型生成样本的多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型和流匹配模型在生成时倾向于产生重复或同质化样本，缺乏多样性，而现有提升多样性的方法往往牺牲质量或需要额外训练。

Method: 利用Fokker-Planck方程的对称性，通过散度自由的漂移扰动改变粒子轨迹但保持边缘分布不变；具体使用基于核的反对称成对矩阵场（由排斥方向构建），实现粒子级的多样性促进。针对计算开销大的情况（如文本到图像生成），提出实用近似。

Result: 在合成分布和文本到图像生成任务上，EDDY在不牺牲分布保真度的前提下显著提升了生成多样性，优于常见基线方法。

Conclusion: EDDY提供了一种无需训练、理论保证边缘分布不变的多样性提升方案，在保持生成质量的同时有效促进样本多样性。

Abstract: We present EDDY (Exact-marginal Diversification via Divergence-free dYnamics), a guidance mechanism for diffusion and flow matching models that promotes diversity among samples generated while maintaining quality. EDDY exploits symmetries of the Fokker-Planck equation, using drift perturbations that change particle trajectories while preserving the evolving marginal distribution. We instantiate this principle through kernel-based anti-symmetric pairwise matrix fields, constructed from the repulsive directions. The resulting divergence-free dynamics promote diversity at the joint particle level while preserving each particle's marginal distribution without any additional training. As computing the guidance can be computationally expensive in cases such as text-to-image generation with perceptual embeddings, we propose practical approximations as an effective and efficient solution. Experiments on synthetic distributions and text-to-image generation show that EDDY improves diversity while maintaining strong distributional fidelity compared to common baselines.

</details>


### [306] [Optimal Counterfactual Search in Tree Ensembles: A Study Across Modeling and Solution Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06561)
*Awa Khouna,Youssouf Emine,Julien Ferry,Thibaut Vidal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了树集成模型下满足可行性和可行动性约束的最优反事实解释问题，提出了CPCF（约束规划）方法，并将之与MaxSAT和MILP范式进行系统比较，实验表明CP整体最优，各范式在不同场景下各有优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实解释的可信度取决于推荐变更是否真正最小化；次优解释可能过度偏离实际所需变化，且启发式错误会不公平地影响不同用户，导致部分用户获得有效解释而另一些被分配不必要的昂贵建议。因此需要计算严格最优的反事实解释。

Method: 将反事实搜索建模为组合优化问题，利用树集成中分裂阈值定义的区间域编码数值特征，离散特征保留原生有限域表示，提出CPCF约束规划公式；同时扩展MaxSAT公式以支持软投票集成，并与现有最优MILP方法进行系统比较。

Result: 在10个数据集和3种树集成类型上，CP整体表现最佳；MaxSAT在硬投票集成上表现优异；MILP在中等分裂深度的摊销推理场景中仍有竞争力。

Conclusion: 不同数学规划范式各有适用场景：CP通用性最强，MaxSAT适合硬投票集成，MILP在特定推理设置下仍具优势，为反事实解释的最优计算方法选择提供了指导。

Abstract: Trust in counterfactual explanations depends critically on whether their recommended changes are truly minimal: suboptimal explanations may vastly overshoot the actual changes needed to alter a decision, and heuristic errors can affect individuals unevenly, giving some users relevant recourse while assigning others unnecessarily costly recommendations. Consequently, we study the problem of computing optimal counterfactual explanations for tree ensembles under plausibility and actionability constraints. This is a combinatorial problem: for a fixed model, counterfactual search boils down to selecting consistent branching decisions and threshold-defined regions under a distance objective. We exploit this structure through CPCF, a constraint programming (CP) formulation in which numerical features are encoded as interval domains induced by split thresholds, while discrete features retain native finite-domain representations. This yields a compact finite-domain formulation that supports multiple distance objectives without continuous split-boundary search. We then place CPCF in a broader comparison across mathematical programming paradigms: we extend a maximum Boolean satisfiability (MaxSAT) formulation, originally designed for hard-voting random forests, to soft-voting ensembles, and compare against the current state-of-the-art mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) optimal approach. Across ten datasets and three types of tree ensembles, we analyze scalability, anytime performance, and sensitivity to distance metrics. We observe that CP achieves the best overall performance. More importantly, our results identify regimes in which the specific strengths of each paradigm make it best suited: CP is most versatile overall, MaxSAT handles hard-voting ensembles particularly well, and MILP remains competitive in amortized inference settings with a moderate number of split levels.

</details>


### [307] [Feature Dimensionality Outweighs Model Complexity in Breast Cancer Subtype Classification Using TCGA-BRCA Gene Expression Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06562)
*Meena Al Hasani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用TCGA-BRCA基因表达数据，评估了模型复杂度（逻辑回归、随机森林、SVM）和特征选择（可变基因数量从50到20,518）对乳腺癌亚型分类性能的影响。结果表明，逻辑回归在亚型级别表现最稳定均衡，包括对稀有类别的检测；随机森林在少数亚型上表现不佳；SVM对特征维度敏感。研究强调了模型简洁性、评估指标和特征选择在高维生物分类任务中的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乳腺癌亚型的准确分类对诊断和治疗选择至关重要，但基因表达数据具有高维度和样本量有限的特点，给机器学习模型带来挑战。现有研究缺乏对模型复杂性、特征选择与亚型级别分类性能之间关系的系统评估。

Method: 使用TCGA-BRCA基因表达数据，训练逻辑回归、随机森林和支持向量机（SVM）三种模型，采用不同数量的高度可变基因（50到20,518个）作为特征。使用分层5折交叉验证进行评估，评估指标包括准确率和宏F1分数。

Result: 所有模型均取得高准确率，但宏F1分析揭示了亚型级别性能的显著差异：逻辑回归在各亚型上表现最稳定均衡，对稀有类别的检测也有所改善；随机森林虽然在整体准确率上表现强劲，但在少数亚型上表现不足；SVM对特征维度敏感。

Conclusion: 模型简洁性、评估指标的选择（尤其是宏F1而非仅有准确率）以及特征选择在高维生物分类任务中至关重要。逻辑回归因其稳定均衡的亚型级性能，可能更适合乳腺癌亚型分类任务。

Abstract: Accurate classification of breast cancer subtypes from gene expression data is critical for diagnosis and treatment selection. However, such datasets are characterized by high dimensionality and limited sample size, posing challenges for machine learning models.
  In this study, we evaluate the impact of model complexity and feature selection on subtype classification performance using TCGA-BRCA gene expression data. Logistic regression, random forest, and support vector machine (SVM) models were trained using varying numbers of highly variable genes (50 to 20,518). Performance was evaluated using stratified 5-fold cross-validation and assessed with accuracy and macro F1 score. While all models achieved high accuracy, macro F1 analysis revealed substantial differences in subtype-level performance. Logistic regression demonstrated the most stable and balanced performance across subtypes, including improved detection of rare classes. Random forest underperformed on minority subtypes despite strong overall accuracy, while SVM showed sensitivity to feature dimensionality. These findings highlight the importance of model simplicity, evaluation metrics, and feature selection in high-dimensional biological classification tasks.

</details>


### [308] [SNAPO: Smooth Neural Adjoint Policy Optimization for Optimal Control via Differentiable Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06570)
*Dmitri Goloubentsev,Natalija Karpichina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SNAPO框架将神经策略嵌入可微模拟器，通过平滑近似处理硬约束，利用伴随方法在一次反向传播中同时计算策略参数和所有输入的精确梯度，在天然气存储、养老金管理和制药生产三个领域展示了极快的训练速度和高效的敏感性计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态规划精确但指数级扩展困难，黑盒强化学习处理高维状态但训练慢且不产生敏感性信息，需要一种既能高效训练又能快速计算敏感性的方法。

Method: 将神经策略嵌入已知的可微模拟器，用平滑近似替代硬约束，通过单次伴随前向传播计算目标函数关于所有策略参数和所有输入的精确梯度。

Result: 天然气存储：不到一分钟训练，365条远期曲线敏感性零额外成本；养老金管理：敏感性计算比bump-and-revalue快6.5-200倍；制药制造：4单元过程链的跨单元敏感性，20个ICH Q8监管敏感性在74.5毫秒内通过5次伴随传播完成。

Conclusion: 所有敏感性均由训练策略的同一反向传播产生，计算成本与一次反向传播成正比，与敏感性数量无关，显著优于传统方法。

Abstract: Many real-world problems require sequential decisions under uncertainty: when to inject or withdraw gas from storage, how to rebalance a pension portfolio each month, what temperature profile to run through a pharmaceutical reactor chain. Dynamic programming solves small instances exactly but scales exponentially in state dimensions. Black-box reinforcement learning handles high-dimensional states but trains slowly and produces no sensitivities. We introduce SNAPO (Smooth Neural Adjoint Policy Optimization), a framework that embeds a neural policy inside a known, differentiable simulator, replaces hard constraints with smooth approximations, and computes exact gradients of the objective with respect to all policy parameters and all inputs in a single adjoint pass. We demonstrate SNAPO on three domains: natural gas storage (training in under a minute, 365 forward curve sensitivities at no additional cost per sensitivity), pension fund asset-liability management (6.5x-200x sensitivity speedup over bump-and-revalue, scaling with the number of risk factors), and pharmaceutical manufacturing (cross-unit sensitivities through a 4-unit process chain, with 20 ICH Q8 regulatory sensitivities from 5 adjoint passes in 74.5 milliseconds). All sensitivities are produced by the same backward pass that trains the policy, at a cost proportional to one reverse pass regardless of how many sensitivities are computed.

</details>


### [309] [Directional Consistency as a Complementary Optimization Signal: The GONO Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06575)
*Victor Daniel Gera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现深度学习优化中存在方向对齐与损失收敛可解耦的现象，并据此提出GONO优化器，通过基于梯度方向一致性动态调整动量系数，在保持理论收敛保证的同时实现更优的优化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化器（如Adam、SGD、RMSprop）缺乏利用梯度方向时序一致性的显式机制，依赖基于幅度的信号，无法有效区分平台区、鞍点和真正收敛，导致方向对齐与损失收敛可能解耦。

Method: 提出GONO（Gradient-Oriented Norm-Adaptive Optimizer），基于连续梯度余弦相似度cc_t动态调整Adam的动量系数beta_1：方向一致时放大动量，振荡时抑制动量。理论上证明其与Adam同阶的O(1/√T)收敛速率，且无信息时退化为Adam。

Result: cc_t在振荡检测上F1=1.00（梯度范数仅0.45）；GONO在MNIST (98.15%)、CIFAR-10 (43.14%)和ResNet-18 (75.44%)上与AdamW保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 方向对齐是一个有理论依据且实践可行的优化信号，GONO验证了其有效性。

Abstract: We identify and formalize an underexplored phenomenon in deep learning optimization: directional alignment and loss convergence can be decoupled. An optimizer can exhibit near-perfect directional consistency (cc_t -> 1, measured via consecutive gradient cosine similarity) while the loss remains high or decreases slowly. This observation reveals that existing optimizers such as Adam, SGD, and RMSprop lack explicit mechanisms to exploit temporal consistency in gradient directions, relying instead on magnitude-based signals that fail to distinguish plateaus, saddle points, and genuine convergence. Motivated by this, we introduce GONO (Gradient-Oriented Norm-Adaptive Optimizer), which adapts Adam's momentum coefficient beta_1 based on cc_t: amplifying momentum under directional consistency and suppressing it during oscillation. We prove GONO matches Adam's O(1/sqrt(T)) convergence rate and reduces exactly to Adam when the signal is uninformative. Empirically, cc_t achieves oscillation detection with F1=1.00 (vs. 0.45 for gradient norm), and GONO remains competitive with AdamW on MNIST (98.15%), CIFAR-10 (43.14%), and ResNet-18 (75.44%), establishing directional alignment as a theoretically grounded, practically actionable optimization signal. Code: https://github.com/victordaniel/gono-optimizer

</details>


### [310] [On the Safety of Graph Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06576)
*Xiaoguang Guo,Zehong Wang,Ziming Li,Shawn Spitzel,Soonwoo Kwon,Tianyi Ma,Yanfang Ye,Chuxu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRL-Safety 是一个多轴安全评估基准，用于系统评估图表示学习方法在五个安全维度（鲁棒性、OOD泛化、类别不平衡、公平性、可解释性）上的表现。通过对12种方法在25个数据集上的标准化评估，发现安全行为由表示设计与压力因素交互决定，基础模型时代的方法呈现轴特定优势而非全面领先，且存在部署能力差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表示学习评估主要关注干净迁移、适应性和任务覆盖，忽视了部署压力（如图信号、图上下文、标签支持、结构群体或预测证据）对方法可靠性的影响，缺乏系统性安全评估框架。

Method: 构建GRL-Safety基准，对12种代表性方法（包括拓扑嵌入、监督GNN、自监督图模型和图基础模型）在25个图数据集上进行标准化评估，覆盖五个安全轴：腐败鲁棒性、OOD泛化、类别不平衡、公平性和可解释性，保留方法原生适应能力，按轴和子条件分别报告结果而非单一聚合分数。

Result: 发现三个跨轴洞见：(1) 安全行为由表示设计与压力因素的交互决定，而非单纯由方法族决定；(2) 基础模型时代的方法呈现轴特定优势而非全面安全主导；(3) 即使在最佳评估方法下，某些部署场景仍然困难，暴露出模型选择之外的新鲁棒性、适应或训练目标需求。

Conclusion: GRL-Safety揭示了现有GRL方法在多种部署压力下的安全性能差异和局限性，为未来研究提供了方向，并开源了基准、评估协议和代码。

Abstract: Graph representation learning (GRL) has evolved from topology-only graph embeddings to task-specific supervised GNNs, and more recently to reusable representations and graph foundation models (GFMs). However, existing evaluations mainly measure clean transfer, adaptation, and task coverage. It remains unclear whether GRL methods stay reliable when deployment stresses affect graph signals, graph contexts, label support, structural groups, or predictive evidence. We introduce GRL-Safety, a multi-axis safety evaluation benchmark for GRL. GRL-Safety evaluates twelve representative methods, spanning topology-only embedding methods, supervised GNNs, self-supervised graph models, and GFMs, on twenty-five graph datasets under standardized evaluation conditions while preserving method-native adaptation. The evaluation covers five safety axes: corruption robustness, OOD generalization, class imbalance, fairness, and interpretation, with per-axis and sub-condition reporting rather than a single aggregate score. Our analysis yields three cross-axis insights that can inspire future research. First, safety behavior is shaped by the interaction between representation design and the stressed graph factor, rather than by method family alone. Second, foundation-era methods show axis-specific strengths rather than broad safety dominance. Third, several deployment regimes remain difficult even for the best evaluated method, revealing capability gaps that require new robustness, adaptation, or training objectives beyond model selection. The benchmark, evaluation protocols, and code are available at: https://github.com/GXG-CS/GRL-Safety.

</details>


### [311] [Distributionally-Robust Learning to Optimize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06585)
*Vinit Ranjan,Jisun Park,Bartolomeo Stellato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分布鲁棒的方法来学习凸优化中一阶方法的超参数（如步长），通过最小化Wasserstein分布鲁棒版本的性能估计问题（PEP），统一了经典学习优化（L2O）和最坏情况最优算法设计两个极端，并在多个基准测试中取得了优于两类基线方法的可认证鲁棒性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的学习优化（L2O）方法在学习算法参数时缺乏对分布偏移的鲁棒性保证，而传统的最坏情况最优算法设计（如PEP）虽然提供了最坏情况下的性能保证，但在平均性能上可能过于保守。作者希望统一这两种范式，在保留数据驱动学习能力的同时提供可认证的鲁棒性能保证。

Method: 1) 提出Wasserstein分布鲁棒性能估计问题（DR-PEP），以数据集中的问题实例为经验分布，在其Wasserstein邻域内最小化最坏情况下的性能；2) 当鲁棒半径趋于0时退化为经典L2O，趋于无穷时退化为最坏情况PEP；3) 通过随机梯度下降求解该问题，每一步需要微分求解一个内部半定规划（SDP）；4) 推导了高概率泛化误差界，证明真实风险不超过样本内L2O最优值加一个随样本量衰减的松弛项，且不劣于最坏情况PEP界。

Result: 在无约束二次极小化、LASSO和线性规划三个基准测试上，学习到的算法在分布外测试集上表现出强鲁棒性，同时优于最坏情况最优算法和普通L2O基线方法。

Conclusion: 分布鲁棒PEP框架有效统一了数据驱动的算法学习与最坏情况性能保证，能够学习到在分布偏移下仍具有可认证鲁棒性的优化算法超参数，为学习优化提供了理论保障与实证优势。

Abstract: We propose a distributionally robust approach to learning hyperparameters for first-order methods in convex optimization. Given a dataset of problem instances, we minimize a Wasserstein distributionally robust version of the performance estimation problem (PEP) over algorithm parameters such as step sizes. Our framework unifies two extremes: as the robustness radius vanishes, we recover classical learning to optimize (L2O); as it grows, we recover worst-case optimal algorithm design via PEP. We solve the resulting problem with stochastic gradient descent, differentiating through the solution of an inner semidefinite program at each step. We prove high-probability bounds showing that the true risk of the learned algorithm is at most the in-sample L2O optimum plus a slack that shrinks with the sample size, and is no worse than the worst-case PEP bound. On unconstrained quadratic minimization, LASSO, and linear programming benchmarks, our learned algorithms achieve strong out-of-sample performance with certifiable robustness, outperforming both worst-case optimal and vanilla L2O baselines.

</details>


### [312] [Towards Metric-Faithful Neural Graph Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06588)
*Jyotirmaya Shivottam,Subhankar Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个连接编码器几何与图编辑距离（GED）估计质量的理论框架，证明了双Lipschitz编码器能改进GED预测和排序性能，并通过FSW-GNN作为即插即用模块在多个基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管神经图匹配架构在GED估计上取得了进展，但编码器几何在其中的作用仍未得到充分理解，缺乏理论指导。

Method: 建立理论框架分析编码器几何对两类GED估计器的影响；提出图级和节点级双Lipschitz条件作为理想几何性质；将FSW-GNN（双Lipschitz、WL等价编码器）作为即插即用替换模块集成到现有架构中。

Result: 在多个代表性基线和基准数据集上，几何感知变体显著提升了GED预测和排序指标；消融实验和迁移实验证实增益来源于改进的表示几何。

Conclusion: 编码器几何应作为神经图匹配的一个有用设计原则，双Lipschitz几何性质可有效改善GED估计质量。

Abstract: Graph Edit Distance (GED) is a fundamental, albeit NP-hard, metric for structural graph similarity. Recent neural graph matching architectures approximate GED by first encoding graphs with a Graph Neural Network (GNN) and then applying either a graph-level regression head or a matching-based alignment module. Despite substantial architectural progress, the role of encoder geometry in neural GED estimation remains poorly understood. In this paper, we develop a theoretical framework that connects encoder geometry to GED estimation quality for two broad classes of neural GED estimators: graph similarity predictors and alignment-based methods. On fixed graph collections, where the doubly-stochastic metric $d_{\mathrm{DS}}$ is comparable to GED, we show that graph-level bi-Lipschitz encoders yield controlled GED surrogates and improved ranking stability; for matching-based estimators, node-level bi-Lipschitz geometry propagates to encoder-induced alignment costs and the resulting optimized alignment objective. We instantiate this perspective using FSW-GNN, a bi-Lipschitz WL-equivalent encoder, as a drop-in replacement in representative neural GED architectures. Across representative baselines and benchmark datasets, the resulting geometry-aware variants significantly improve GED prediction and ranking metrics. A faithfulness case study of untrained encoders, together with ablations and transfer experiments, supports the view that these gains arise from improved representation geometry, positioning encoder geometry as a useful design principle for neural graph matching.

</details>


### [313] [Weight-Decay Turns Transformer Loss Landscapes Villani: Functional-Analytic Foundations for Optimization and Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06599)
*Abhijit Das,Sayantan Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次从泛函分析角度严格证明了带L2正则化（权重衰减）的Transformer目标函数满足Villani的强制性能量函数准则，推导出对数Sobolev和Poincaré常数，并通过大规模实验验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权重衰减在大型语言模型中广泛使用，但其在塑造Transformer损失景观中的理论作用尚未得到充分探索。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 1) 证明正则化损失函数满足Villani强制性能量函数的四个条件（无限可微、二次增长、高斯可积尾部、微分增长条件）；2) 推导显式的对数Sobolev和Poincaré常数；3) 提出可扩展的Villani诊断量Ψ_s，使用Hutchinson迹探针高效估计。

Result: GPT-Neo-125M在Penn Treebank和WikiText-103上的实验验证了：Ψ_s的二次增长、Hessian谱膨胀、以及对数Sobolev分析一致的指数收敛行为。

Conclusion: 权重衰减不仅凭经验改善了泛化性能，还为快速Langevin混合和曲率感知优化建立了数学条件，为深度学习提供了理论基础的优化方法。

Abstract: Weight decay is widely used as a regularizer in large language models, yet its precise role in shaping Transformer loss landscapes remains theoretically underexplored. This paper provides the first rigorous functional-analytic characterization of the standard Transformer objective--cross-entropy loss with $L^2$ regularization--by proving it satisfies Villani's criteria for coercive energy functions. Specifically, we show that the regularized loss $\mathcal{F}$ is infinitely differentiable, grows at least quadratically, has Gaussian-integrable tails, and satisfies the differential growth condition $-Δ\mathcal{F} + \tfrac{1}{s}\|\nabla\mathcal{F}\|^{2} \to \infty$ as $\|θ\| \to \infty$ for all $s>0$. From this structure, we derive explicit log-Sobolev and Poincaré constants $C_{\mathrm{LS}} \leq λ^{-1} + d/λ^{2}$, linking the regularization strength $λ$ and model dimension $d$ to finite-time convergence guarantees for noisy stochastic gradient descent and PAC-Bayesian generalization bounds that tighten with increasing $λ$. To validate our theory, we introduce a scalable Villani diagnostic $Ψ_s(θ) = -Δ\mathcal{F} + s^{-1}\|\nabla \mathcal{F}\|^2$ and estimate it efficiently using Hutchinson trace probes in models with over 100M parameters. Experiments on GPT-Neo-125M across Penn Treebank and WikiText-103 confirm the predicted quadratic growth of $Ψ_s$, spectral inflation of the Hessian, and exponential convergence behavior consistent with our log-Sobolev analysis. These results demonstrate that weight decay not only improves generalization empirically but also establishes the mathematical conditions required for fast Langevin mixing and theoretically grounded curvature-aware optimization in deep learning.

</details>


### [314] [How Many Iterations to Jailbreak? Dynamic Budget Allocation for Multi-Turn LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06605)
*Shai Feldman,Yaniv Romano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DAPRO框架，首个理论上有效的动态预算分配方法，用于多轮LLM交互中事件发生时间的边界预测，在满足预算约束的同时提供分布自由、有限样本的覆盖保证，且覆盖边界比先前工作更紧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮对话场景下评估LLM性能计算昂贵，关键事件（如越狱攻击、任务完成）往往在多次交互后才出现且可能很罕见，传统静态预算分配效率低下。

Method: 提出动态预算分配框架DAPRO（Dynamic Allocation via PRojected Optimization），在满足预算约束的前提下动态分配计算资源。理论贡献包括：(1) 无需条件独立假设的分布自由覆盖保证；(2) 基于平均删失权重的平方根而非最坏情况权重的更紧覆盖边界。

Result: 在Llama 3.1和Qwen 2.5等模型上的智能体任务成功、越狱攻击、有毒内容生成和RAG幻觉等实验中，DAPRO相比静态基线更接近名义覆盖水平、方差更低，且满足预算约束。

Conclusion: DAPRO为多轮LLM交互评估提供了高效、理论上有效的动态预算分配方案，兼具覆盖保证和计算效率优势。

Abstract: Evaluating and predicting the performance of large language models (LLMs) in multi-turn conversational settings is critical yet computationally expensive; key events -- e.g., jailbreaks or successful task completion by an agent -- often emerge only after repeated interactions. These events might be rare, and under any feasible computational budget, remain unobserved.
  Recent conformal survival frameworks construct reliable lower predictive bounds (LPBs) on the number of iterations to trigger the event of interest, but rely on static budget allocation that is inefficient in multi-turn setups. To address this, we introduce \emph{Dynamic Allocation via PRojected Optimization} (DAPRO), the first theoretically valid dynamic budget allocation framework for bounding the time-to-event in multi-turn LLM interactions.
  We prove that DAPRO satisfies the budget constraint and provides distribution-free, finite-sample coverage guarantees without requiring the conditional independence between censoring and event times assumed by prior conformal survival approaches.
  A key theoretical contribution is a novel coverage bound that scales with the square root of the mean censoring weight rather than the worst-case weight, yielding provably tighter guarantees than prior work. Furthermore, DAPRO can be employed to obtain unbiased, low-variance estimates of population-level evaluation metrics, such as the jailbreak rate, under limited computing resources.
  Comprehensive experiments across agentic task success, adversarial jailbreaks, toxic content generation, and RAG hallucinations using LLMs such as Llama 3.1 and Qwen 2.5 demonstrate that DAPRO consistently achieves coverage closer to the nominal level with lower variance than static baselines, while satisfying the budget constraint.

</details>


### [315] [Transformers Efficiently Perform In-Context Logistic Regression via Normalized Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06609)
*Chenyang Zhang,Yuan Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了带softmax注意力的Transformer在线性分类数据上的上下文学习机制。作者构建了一类多层Transformer，每层精确执行一步归一化梯度下降，从而实现在上下文中进行逻辑回归。通过单层自注意力的单步梯度下降训练和循环堆叠，可以构造出这样的Transformer，并提供了训练收敛和分布外泛化的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Transformer如何通过隐式执行上下文中的算法来展现强大的上下文学习能力，特别是softmax注意力机制在线性分类任务中的工作机制。

Method: (1) 构建多层Transformer结构，每层对应一步归一化梯度下降；(2) 通过单层自注意力层在单步梯度下降监督下训练；(3) 循环堆叠训练好的层得到循环模型，实现多步优化。

Result: 成功构造了能执行上下文逻辑回归的Transformer，提供了单层自注意力训练收敛的理论保证，以及循环模型在分布外泛化上的理论保证。

Conclusion: 展示了softmax Transformer可以有效地作为上下文学习器，并通过梯度下降的视角为ICL机制提供了理论解释，推进了对Transformer上下文学习能力的理论理解。

Abstract: Transformers have demonstrated remarkable in-context learning (ICL) capabilities. The strong ICL performance of transformers is commonly believed to arise from their ability to implicitly execute certain algorithms on the context, thereby enhancing prediction and generation. In this work, we investigate how transformers with softmax attention perform in-context learning on linear classification data. We first construct a class of multi-layer transformers that can perform in-context logistic regression, with each layer exactly performing one step of normalized gradient descent on an in-context loss. Then, we show that our constructed transformer can be obtained through (i) training a single self-attention layer supervised by one-step gradient descent, and (ii) recurrently applying the trained layer to obtain a looped model. Training convergence guarantees of the self-attention layer and out-of-distribution generalization guarantees of the looped model are provided. Our results advance the theoretical understanding of ICL mechanism by showcasing how softmax transformers can effectively act as in-context learners.

</details>


### [316] [The Structural Origin of Attention Sink: Variance Discrepancy, Super Neurons, and Dimension Disparity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06611)
*Siquan Li,Kaiqi Jiang,Jiacheng Sun,Tianyang Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM中注意力集中现象的机制：源于自注意力中的值聚合过程导致的方差差异，并通过FFN层中超级神经元的激活被急剧放大。通过两种干预实验验证了因果关系，并提出了head-wise RMSNorm架构修改来稳定预训练过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管注意力集中现象在LLM中普遍存在，但其结构起源尚不明确，本文旨在提供对这一现象的机制性解释。

Method: (1) 追溯注意力集中源于自注意力值聚合过程中的系统性方差差异；(2) 证明FFN层中超级神经元激活会放大该差异；(3) 通过注意力掩码修改和放大目标token表示方差两种干预实验验证因果链；(4) 提出head-wise RMSNorm架构修改。

Result: 两种干预实验均能在任意位置复制注意力集中现象；head-wise RMSNorm能恢复位置间的统计均衡，显著加速预训练收敛。

Conclusion: 提供了对注意力集中现象的机制性理解，为系统性控制集中形成奠定基础，并通过架构修改证明了恢复统计均衡的有效性。

Abstract: Despite the prevalence of the attention sink phenomenon in Large Language Models (LLMs), where initial tokens disproportionately monopolize attention scores, its structural origins remain elusive. This work provides a \textit{mechanistic explanation} for this phenomenon. First, we trace its root to the value aggregation process inherent in self-attention, which induces a systematic variance discrepancy. We further demonstrate that this discrepancy is drastically amplified by the activation of super neurons within Feed-Forward Network (FFN) layers. Specifically, the channel-sparse down-projections trigger a dimension disparity of the first-token representation, necessitating the formation of attention sinks as a structural anchor. Then, we validate this causal chain through two controlled interventions: (i) isolating the aggregation effect via attention mask modifications and (ii) amplifying the variance of targeted token representations. Both interventions can replicate attention sinks at arbitrary positions. Our mechanistic understanding offers a foundation for the systematic control of sink formation. Finally, as a proof of concept, we propose \textit{head-wise RMSNorm}, an architectural modification that stabilizes value aggregation outputs during pre-training. Our experiments demonstrate that restoring statistical parity across positions significantly accelerates convergence.

</details>


### [317] [Online Bayesian Calibration under Gradual and Abrupt System Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06612)
*Yang Xu,Chiwoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出贝叶斯递归投影校准（BRPC），一种面向流数据的在线贝叶斯校准框架，通过分离无差异粒子更新与条件高斯过程差异更新，并结合重启机制应对渐变漂移和突变，解决了传统离线校准中的参数-差异混淆和非平稳性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝叶斯校准存在参数-差异混淆问题，且作为离线过程假设数据平稳，无法适应数字孪生中系统随时间演化、发生渐变漂移或突变的需求；数据同化方法虽能序贯更新，但通常不显式建模系统偏差，在突变情况下效果不佳。

Method: BRPC将投影校准扩展到在线场景：（1）对校准参数进行无差异粒子更新，对差异函数进行条件高斯过程更新，两者分离以保证可辨识性；（2）集成重启机制检测突变并重置校准过程，分别处理渐变演化和突变。

Result: 在合成数据和植物仿真基准上的实验表明，BRPC在渐变变化下提高了校准精度；重启增强版BRPC在突变下相比滑窗贝叶斯校准和数据同化基线，进一步提升了鲁棒性和预测性能。

Conclusion: BRPC为数字孪生场景下非平稳流数据的在线贝叶斯校准提供了理论保证和有效方案，兼顾偏差建模与参数可辨识性，能够同时处理渐变和突变两种系统演化模式。

Abstract: Bayesian model calibration is central to digital twins and computer experiments, as it aligns model outputs with field observations by estimating calibration parameters and correcting systematic model bias. Classical Bayesian calibration introduces latent parameters and a discrepancy function to model bias, but suffers from parameter--discrepancy confounding and is typically formulated as an offline procedure under a stationary data-generating assumption. These limitations are restrictive in modern digital twin applications, where systems evolve over time and may exhibit gradual drift and abrupt regime shifts. While data assimilation methods enable sequential updates, they generally do not explicitly model systematic bias and are less effective under abrupt changes. We propose Bayesian Recursive Projected Calibration (BRPC), an online Bayesian calibration framework for streaming data under simulator mismatch and nonstationarity. BRPC extends projected calibration to the online setting by separating a discrepancy-free particle update for calibration parameters from a conditional Gaussian process update for discrepancy, preserving identifiability while enabling bias-aware adaptation under gradual system evolution. To handle abrupt changes, BRPC is integrated with restart mechanisms that detect regime shifts and reset the calibration process. We establish theoretical guarantees for both components, including tracking performance under gradual evolution and false-alarm and detection behavior for restart mechanisms. Empirical studies on synthetic and plant-simulation benchmarks show that BRPC improves calibration accuracy under gradual changes, while restart-augmented BRPC further improves robustness and predictive performance under abrupt regime shifts compared to sliding-window Bayesian calibration and data assimilation baselines.

</details>


### [318] [Concept-Based Abductive and Contrastive Explanations for Behaviors of Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06640)
*Ronaldo Canizales,Divya Gopinath,Corina Păsăreanu,Ravi Mangal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出概念性溯因与对比解释，融合概念解释与形式化溯因/对比解释，利用概念擦除建立因果关系，枚举最小概念集以解释模型在单张和集合图像上的预测行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念解释方法要么未建立概念与预测间的因果联系，要么表达能力有限（仅单概念）；而形式化溯因/对比解释虽能计算因果相关的最小输入特征集，但仅考虑像素等低级特征。两者未能有效结合。

Method: 提出概念性溯因与对比解释，定义最小高级概念集；使用概念擦除程序建立因果关系；设计算法族枚举所有最小解释；通过聚合解释理解单张图像及具有共同行为的图像集合上的模型预测。

Result: 在多个模型、数据集和行为上验证了该方法在计算有用且用户友好的解释方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 概念性溯因与对比解释能有效弥合概念解释与形式化解释之间的鸿沟，提供兼具因果性和可表达性的解释方案。

Abstract: *Concept-based explanations* offer a promising approach for explaining the predictions of deep neural networks in terms of high-level, human-understandable concepts. However, existing methods either do not establish a causal connection between the concepts and model predictions or are limited in expressivity and only able to infer causal explanations involving single concepts. At the same time, the parallel line of work on *formal abductive and contrastive explanations* computes the minimal set of input features causally relevant for model outcomes but only considers low-level features such as pixels. Merging these two threads, in this work, we propose the notion of *concept-based abductive and contrastive explanations* that capture the minimal sets of high-level concepts causally relevant for model outcomes. We then present a family of algorithms that enumerate all minimal explanations while using *concept erasure* procedures to establish causal relationships. By appropriately aggregating such explanations, we are not only able to understand model predictions on individual images but also on collections of images where the model exhibits a user-specified, common *behavior*. We evaluate our approach on multiple models, datasets, and behaviors, and demonstrate its effectiveness in computing helpful, user-friendly explanations.

</details>


### [319] [Inductive Venn-Abers and related regressors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06646)
*Ivan Petej,Vladimir Vovk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将Venn-Abers预测器从二元分类和有界回归推广到无界回归场景，通过引入共形预测元素实现。实验表明，基于Venn-Abers回归器导出的点回归器在大训练集上能一定程度提升标准回归器的预测效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Venn-Abers预测器具有有效性保证但仅适用于二元分类和有界回归，缺乏对无界回归场景的支持，限制了其应用范围。

Method: 将Venn-Abers预测器推广到无界回归，通过引入共形预测(conformal prediction)元素来处理无界情形。

Result: 模拟和实证研究表明，基于Venn-Abers回归器导出的点回归器在较大训练集上能够在一定程度上提升标准回归器的预测效率。

Conclusion: Venn-Abers预测器可成功推广至无界回归场景，且在大训练集上具有提升预测效率的潜力。

Abstract: Venn-Abers predictors are probabilistic predictors that enjoy appealing properties of validity, but their major limitation is that they are applicable only to the case of binary classification, with a recent extension to bounded regression. We generalize them to the case of unbounded regression, which requires adding an element of conformal prediction. In our simulation and empirical studies we investigate the predictive efficiency of point regressors derived from Venn-Abers regressors and argue that they somewhat improve the predictive efficiency of standard regressors for larger training sets.

</details>


### [320] [Optimizer-Model Consistency: Full Finetuning with the Same Optimizer as Pretraining Forgets Less](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06654)
*Yuxing Liu,Jianyu Wang,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“优化器-模型一致性”现象：在监督微调（SFT）阶段，使用与预训练相同的优化器（如AdamW）相比使用其他优化器或LoRA，能实现更好的学习-遗忘权衡。通过实验和理论分析，发现优化器通过正则化效应塑造模型激活分布，而相同的优化器在SFT中能产生特定结构的权重更新以减少遗忘。此外，Muon优化器在推理任务微调中表现较差，因其在少量数据上倾向于死记硬背。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在LLM监督微调阶段，优化器选择对学习新任务与遗忘预训练知识之间权衡的影响，特别是发现使用与预训练相同的优化器能取得更好效果这一现象。

Method: 通过受控实验和理论分析，研究优化器对模型激活的正则化效应，以及权重更新结构对遗忘的影响。对比Muon和AdamW在预训练和SFT全流程中的表现，并设计合成语言建模实验验证Muon的记忆倾向。

Result: 1) 使用与预训练相同的优化器进行全量微调（优化器-模型一致性）在遗忘更少的同时能达到相同或更好的新任务性能；2) 优化器通过正则化效应塑造模型的不同损失景观；3) Muon在推理任务微调中表现较差，因其倾向于死记硬背而非学习模式。

Conclusion: 优化器-模型一致性是SFT中值得关注的现象，相同优化器的正则化效应有利于保留预训练知识。Muon虽在预训练中有优势，但在小数据量微调中因过拟合记忆模式而表现不佳。

Abstract: Optimizers play an important role in both pretraining and finetuning stages when training large language models (LLMs). In this paper, we present an observation that full finetuning with the same optimizer as in pretraining achieves a better learning-forgetting tradeoff, i.e., forgetting less while achieving the same or better performance on the new task, than other optimizers and, possibly surprisingly, LoRA, during the supervised finetuning (SFT) stage. We term this phenomenon optimizer-model consistency. To better understand it, through controlled experiments and theoretical analysis, we show that: 1) optimizers can shape the models by having regularization effects on the activations, leading to different landscapes around the pretrained checkpoints; 2) in response to this regularization effect, the weight update in SFT should follow some specific structures to lower forgetting of the knowledge learned in pretraining, which can be obtained by using the same optimizer. Moreover, we specifically compare Muon and AdamW when they are employed throughout the pretraining and SFT stages and find that Muon performs worse when finetuned for reasoning tasks. With a synthetic language modeling experiment, we demonstrate that this can come from Muon's strong tendency towards rote memorization, which may hurt pattern acquisition with a small amount of data, as for SFT.

</details>


### [321] [UniPool: A Globally Shared Expert Pool for Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06665)
*Minbin Huang,Han Shi,Chuanyang Zheng,Yimeng Wu,Guoxuan Chen,Xintong Yu,Yichun Yin,Hong Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UniPool提出了一种共享专家池的MoE架构，取代了传统每层独立专家的设计。通过全局专家池、池级辅助损失和NormRouter，UniPool在多个模型规模上持续优于标准MoE，并且仅用41.6%-66.7%的专家参数就能匹配或超越基线性能，证明专家参数可以随深度亚线性增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MoE架构中每个transformer层拥有独立的专家集，导致深度扩展与专家参数呈线性增长。作者通过路由探针实验发现，将深层学习型top-k路由器替换为均匀随机路由，下游准确率仅下降1.0-1.6个点，表明深层存在明显的专家容量冗余，因此需要重新设计专家分配规则。

Method: UniPool用单个共享专家池替代每层独立的专家集，各层通过独立的路由器访问该池。为实现稳定平衡训练，引入池级辅助损失以平衡整个池的专家利用率，并采用NormRouter提供稀疏且尺度稳定的路由。同时将池大小作为显式的深度缩放超参数。

Result: 在五个LLaMA架构模型规模（182M到978M参数）上训练30B tokens，UniPool持续改进验证损失和困惑度，验证损失最高降低0.0386。缩减池变体仅用41.6%-66.7%的普通专家参数预算就能匹配或超越层式MoE。

Conclusion: 在共享池设计下，专家参数不必随深度线性增长，可以亚线性增长同时保持比标准MoE更高的效率和有效性。UniPool的优势还能与细粒度专家分解互补组合。

Abstract: Modern Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures allocate expert capacity through a rigid per-layer rule: each transformer layer owns a separate expert set. This convention couples depth scaling with linear expert-parameter growth and assumes that every layer needs isolated expert capacity. However, recent analyses and our routing probe challenge this allocation rule: replacing a deeper layer's learned top-k router with uniform random routing drops downstream accuracy by only 1.0-1.6 points across multiple production MoE models. Motivated by this redundancy, we propose UniPool, an MoE architecture that treats expert capacity as a global architectural budget by replacing per-layer expert ownership with a single shared pool accessed by independent per-layer routers. To enable stable and balanced training under sharing, we introduce a pool-level auxiliary loss that balances expert utilization across the entire pool, and adopt NormRouter to provide sparse and scale-stable routing into the shared expert pool. Across five LLaMA-architecture model scales (182M, 469M, 650M, 830M, and 978M parameters) trained on 30B tokens from the Pile, UniPool consistently improves validation loss and perplexity over the matched vanilla MoE baselines. Across these scales, UniPool reduces validation loss by up to 0.0386 relative to vanilla MoE. Beyond raw loss improvement, our results identify pool size as an explicit depth-scaling hyperparameter: reduced-pool UniPool variants using only 41.6%-66.7% of the vanilla expert-parameter budget match or outperform layer-wise MoE at the tested scales. This shows that, under a shared-pool design, expert parameters need not grow linearly with depth; they can grow sublinearly while remaining more efficient and effective than vanilla MoE. Further analysis shows that UniPool's benefits compose with finer-grained expert decomposition.

</details>
